### Ndepụta Nlele Maka Ime Ka Nchedo Sie Ike

```bash
# Opekempe nchekwa production:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # HTTPS dị mkpa
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Nyochaa njirimara oku proxy niile
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Ekpughela key na UI ma ọlị
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Oke 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Nhichapụ Ihe Ntinye & Nchedo PII

OmniRoute na-enye nchedo nwere ọkwa abụọ: nyocha ntụnye ọjọọ n'akụkụ arịrịọ na iwepụ PII n'akụkụ nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ Oke ya:** Usoro nchedo ndị a bụ nchọpụta _heuristic nke a na-agba mbọ ime nke ọma_, ọ bụghị firewall zuru oke megide ntụnye ọjọọ na prompt ma ọ bụ sistemụ PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta nchọpụta ụgha (dịka ịkọpụta prompt persona/RPG na-enweghị nsogbu dịka nke ọjọọ) na enweghị nchọpụta mgbe ihe ọjọọ dị (dịka leetspeak, oghere dị iche iche, na usoro na-abụghị Bekee). Naanị ha ezughị maka irube isi n'ụkpụrụ. Hazie mode ndị ahụ ma nwalee ha megide traffic gị tupu ịdabere na ha.

### N'akụkụ Arịrịọ: Nchedo Megide Prompt Injection

| Variable                          | Nke Ndabara  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Na-eme ka nyocha ozi ndị na-abata maka usoro prompt injection rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                               |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Iwu injection: `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = jụ arịrịọ site na 400. `redact` ochie **anaghị** ewepụ ederede injection; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka idegharị PII dị n'arịrịọ. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo ihe ize ndụ kacha nta nke `MODE=block` ga-ajụ: `high` (ndabara), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-elele usoro `medium` naanị ma ọ bụrụ na e belataghị ya.                           |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                             |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, zoo PII n'arịrịọ ndị na-abata (ọ naghị adabere na mode injection).                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Zoo usoro API-key / secret-token ndị a ma ama na payload arịrịọ/nzaghachi. A ga-ahọrọ ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ; ọ na-eme dịka `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                        |

### N'akụkụ Nzaghachi: Ihe Nhichapụ PII

| Variable                         | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite         | Nkọwa                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`     | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII gbapụrụ tupu iziga ya nye client.             |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact`    | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = zoo PII, `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = tụfuo nzaghachi ahụ dum. |

### Ihe Nhichapụ Context nke Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Variable                            | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`         | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Na-ewepụ context implicit nke active-editor (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) n'arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` ma zoo ọdịnaya faịlụ nwere mmetụta dị elu ndị e tinyere n'ụzọ doro anya. Ọ dị nchebe na ndabara; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. |

### Ọnọdụ Ojiji

| Ọnọdụ Ojiji                    | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Irube isi n'ụkpụrụ ụlọ ọrụ** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (igbochi injection + izochi PII dị n'arịrịọ/nzaghachi; mode ndị ahụ anaghị adabere ibe ha) |
| **Nlekota naanị**              | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — na-edekọ mana ọ naghị egbochi mgbe ọ bụla                                                                                                                 |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**              | Hapụ ha niile ka ha ghara ịrụ ọrụ — enweghị overhead ọ bụla                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 6. Atumatu Ngwaọrụ & Nduzi Ụzọ

| Agbanwe                                                     | Ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                    | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                    | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM nwere iji kpọọ ngwaọrụ/ọrụ. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ ndị edepụtara, `denylist` = ha niile ma e wezụga ndị edepụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mmachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`  | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`        | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ JSON nke iwu mmegharị payload (nhazi upstream maka model/protocol ọ bụla).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`        | Oge etiti (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu payload ọzọ ozugbo mgbanwe mere. Nke kacha nta bụ `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                       | `open-sse/services/model.ts`               | Nhọrọ a na-ahọrọ: duzie ID model `claude-*` ndị na-enweghị prefix sitere na ndị ahịa Claude Code site na akaụntụ OAuth nke Claude Code kama ịmanye ka e nwee prefix provider. Prefix provider e nyere kpọmkwem ka ga-ebute ụzọ. A pụkwara ịhazi ya site na toggle dị na dashboard n’ihu provider Claude.                                                                                                                |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                           | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`         | Oke concurrency maka model ọ bụla n’ime combo round-robin (#9100). E debere semaphore nke combo round-robin n’ụzọ siri ike ka ọ bụrụ arịrịọ 3 na-aga n’otu oge maka model ọ bụla, na-enweghị ụzọ ịgbanwe ya, nke mere ka traffic nwere concurrency dị elu kwụ n’ahịrị n’azụ oke ahụ. A na-enyocha ya ka ọ bụrụ `>= 1`, ma na-amachi ya ka ọ ghara ịgafe `<= 32`.                                                        |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                       | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`            | Nhọrọ dị ize ndụ nke na-awụpụ nyocha context-window / max-input-token nke OmniRoute maka arịrịọ ozugbo gaa na otu model. Ndị provider upstream ka na-amanye oke ha n’ezie; mkpakọ prompt na oke output-token nke model n’onwe ya ka na-arụ ọrụ. Usoro ibu ụzọ dị irè bụ mgbanwe Feature Flags DB > agbanwe gburugburu > ndabara; achọghị ịmalitegharị.                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`     | Akwụkwọ YAML `providers:` dị n’ahịrị (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe edobere ya (ma ọ nwere ma ọ bụ na o nweghị block `strategy:`), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na entry jikọrọ ọnụ nke self-hosted kwekọrọ na OpenAI kama iji pipeline cloud. Mgbe edobeghị ya (nke bụ ndabara): route ahụ na-aga ozugbo na pipeline cloud dị ugbu a. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`     | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nwere otu akwụkwọ `providers:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka deployment ndị na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa env var dị n’ahịrị. Nke ọ bụla n’ime ha na-eme ka entry self-hosted rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(edobeghị — route ghe oghe)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`     | API key nkekọrịta nke a na-ahọrọ maka entry self-hosted jikọrọ ọnụ (ntọala D5, e debere maka usoro quota kwa key). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ ga-etinyerịrị `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Mgbe edobeghị ya: route ahụ ghe oghe, kwekọọ na usoro provider local self-hosted dị ugbu a (deployment loopback/netwọk a tụkwasịrị obi).                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`   | Akwụkwọ YAML `strategy:` dị n’ahịrị maka engine nduzi ụzọ deterministic (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, cooldown breaker, ibute ọnụ ahịa ụzọ, ịtụle latency, usoro fallback. Ọ na-akagbu block `strategy:` dị n’ahịrị nke dị n’ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka key ọ bụla. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`   | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nwere otu akwụkwọ `strategy:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka deployment ndị na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa env var dị n’ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachi nsonaazụ a na-adịghị esochi tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ nyochaa akụkọ ozi, nke a metụtakwara ID nnọkọ ndị ahịa nyere. Otu e si ejikwa nnọkọ ntụgharị ụzọ agbanweghị, a naghịkwa ehichapụ ndekọ ndị dị adị. Nke a bụ maka nrụnye ndị na-adịghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl ntụgharị kwụsị ịba ụba. |

---

## 7. URL na Mmekọrịta Cloud

| Agbanwe                                  | Ndabara                                         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL dị n'akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta dị n'ime na-eji akpọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dịka URL loopback/konteena ọbụlagodi mgbe a na-eji proxy mee ka ngwa ahụ dị n'ihu ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(tọgbọ chakoo)_                                | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL endpoint nke cloud relay (atụmatụ premium).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Oge HTTP ga-echere tupu ọ kwụsị arịrịọ mmekọrịta cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                          | Nhazi build Webpack                              | Profaịlụ oge build (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modul nwere ikike pụrụ iche n'ụzọ anụ ahụ na bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _mmepụta standalone `.build/`_                  | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Ndochi oge build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro colocation mgbe build gachara na-eji. Ọ bụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(tọgbọ chakoo)_                                | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Nzuzo e kere òkè nke a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka endpoint Cloud Sync dochie credentials mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE` | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Cloud Sync na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na atọọghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị flag a. Ndabara ga-agbanwe gaa na mmanye na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`   | `false`                                         | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi na omume otu nzọụkwụ nke v3.8.5, "bubata ihe niile", na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                   | `http://localhost:20128`                        | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọha emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị emepụtara kwesịrị iji canonical reverse-proxy host.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                  | _(tọgbọ chakoo)_                                | Akụkụ klayent                                    | Oyiri `CLOUD_URL` dị n'akụkụ klayent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                    | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                       | _(edobeghị — na-adaghachi na `PORT`)_           | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Port ndabere n'akụkụ klayenti maka URL ngosi mgbe a maghị origin (SSR/ule); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`              | _(edobeghị)_                                    | Onye na-achọpụta origin ọha, URL onyonyo         | Origin OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-ahụ, nke nwere mkpa kachasị elu, a na-eji emepụta URL ọha na nyochaa origin ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-erute OmniRoute site na URL ime, mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-ebudata mgbasa ozi emepụtara site na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ origin ọha. Etinyekwala `/v1`.                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`        | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | URL zuru ezu nke manifest plugin provider a na-akpọsa nye klayenti sidecar. Mgbe edoghị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na origin arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`              | `http`                                          | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin provider site na HOST/PORT n'enweghị origin arịrịọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ proxy ọha na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                  | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ọnọdụ ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka header origin ọha ezigara n'ihu. Edobeghị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto ezigara n'ihu obi naanị site na proxy loopback e tinyere token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa peer proxy LAN nkeonwe obi, ma na-ekwe ka bridge `API_PORT` debe `X-Forwarded-For` nke peer dị otu ahụ. N'ebe production nọ, họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` doro anya.             |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                       | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL callback ọha maka ọrụ kie.ai asynchronous. Override kacha mkpa tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`             | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ụzọ ọzọ e si dee `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. Ọ na-alaghachi na nke a mgbe edoghị variable bụ isi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                   | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Origin ọha a na-eji emepụta URL callback asynchronous. Nhọrọ ndabere kacha ala maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dịka URL ọha izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`               | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint nyocha oke ojiji CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`           | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`           | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go gọọmentị nke API key na-eme authentication, nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`        | `true`                                          | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Mepụta header njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) n'arịrịọ upstream opencode-go/zen nke klayenti eziteghị, ka Cloudflare na egress VPS nwee ike ịnakwere ha (#6210/#5997). Agbanyere ya na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                    | `opencode/1.18.31`                              | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent ndabara a na-eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` gbanyere ma edoghị override `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` maka provider ọ bụla. A na-etinye ya naanị na executor opencode. N'arịrịọ na-enweghị key nke upstream na-egbochi, a ga-eji ndabara a dochie uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` kama ịjụ ya.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                        | `desktop`                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye isi `x-opencode-client` a mepụtara mgbe agbanyere `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                       | `global`                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye isi `x-opencode-project` a mepụtara mgbe agbanyere `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`     | _(edozighị)_                                    | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Nchịkọta ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị irè nke a na-ekewa ọdịda upstream ha dịka IP ọpụpụ si dị (otu 429 na-adọba njikọ ibe ya niile na-ekekọrịta IP ahụ). Ọ bụrụ na edozighị ya, ọ na-eji ndabara ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ usoro ọdịda ahụ kpamkpam; ndepụta e ji rikoma kewaa na-anọchi nchịkọta ahụ. A na-ekewa atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ n'ọkwa akaụntụ na upstream, ya mere enwere ike ịgbanyụ ya n'enweghị nsogbu (#9611). |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`    | _(edozighị)_                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji kwụsị imegharị body nke arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị key (ọkọlọtọ streaming na ndepụta tool). A ka na-etinye nkụnye isi. A na-agụ ya n'arịrịọ ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`   | _(edozighị)_                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha tool e ji rikoma kewaa nke a ga-ekwupụta n'arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị key nke na-enweghị tool ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ na-alaghachi n'otu placeholder a gwara model ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ kachasị ntinye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ntinye na-ezighị ezi anya.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`       | `https://ollama.com/settings`                   | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud eji enweta data oke ojiji. Gbanwee ya maka relay / ihe nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                    | _(edozighị)_                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` eji enweta data oke ojiji site na ibe ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`              | _(edozighị)_                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`          | _(edozighị)_                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ọzọ a na-eji tupu aha mkpirisi ndị ahụ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com` | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Ebe njedebe oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relay / ihe nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebunye ya n'azụ reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike nke gị (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe callback OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha emepụtara ga-eji aha host ahụ. Na-enweghị nke a, callback OAuth nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba, njikọ ọha emepụtara nwekwara ike ịrụtụ aka na origin dị n'ime container.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dịka URL loopback/container dị n'ime maka ọrụ server-to-server. Ejila `Origin` nke ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ aha host ọha maka self-fetch dị n'ime nke nwere credential.
>
> Ide ihe na dashboard nke enyochala njirimara anaghị achọ URL ntọala ọha kwụ ọtọ: dashboard na-eziga arịrịọ same-origin na-adịghị nchebe site na token CSRF ejikọtara na session. OmniRoute ka na-achịkọta nkwado public-origin n'otu ebe maka njikọ ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị env var URL ọha akọwapụtara obi; a na-eleghara nkụnye isi `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` anya ma e wezụga mgbe agbanyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma jiri token kaa proxy peer ozugbo dịka nke a tụkwasịrị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ dashboard same-origin; CORS bụ naanị maka ndị ahịa ihe nchọgharị cross-origin.

---

## 8. Proxy Ọpụpụ

Ziga oku ndị na-enye LLM dị n'elu site na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka njikwa ọpụpụ, nhazi ụzọ dabere na mpaghara, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` na-eji `true` dịka ndabara. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ
`off` iji manye HTTP/1.1 na ndị Undici dispatcher dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: ọpụpụ ozugbo, proxy HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relay, na
nnwale ọzọ ha na-eme site na socket ọhụrụ. Nke a na-echekwa nkwenye asambodo TLS, njirimara
proxy, oke njikọ, na ntọala keep-alive. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe
ị gbanwere ya n'ihi na a na-edobe ndị dispatcher na cache, a na-emepụtakwa ndị relay agent mgbe usoro malitere.
Mgbanwe a anaghị achịkwa wreq TLS fingerprinting ma ọ bụ usoro mbufe ndị akọwapụtara maka onye na-enye ọrụ.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`        | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwado onye nnọchi anya SOCKS5 maka oku ndị na-aga n'elu. Jiri `false` jụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`        | Akụkụ klayent                                  | Ime ka akụkụ klayent mara na SOCKS5 dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`        | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ agbanyere na ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala DB nke dashboard ga-emeri ya). Ọdọ ndị nnọchi anya na ntụgharị n'otu akaụntụ na-akwụsị ịhọrọgharị onye otu dara ogbenye n'oge na-adịbeghị anya (nyocha TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 sitere na ya) ruo oge nke na-amụba okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọ mere ọzọ, ruo n'ókè kachasị. `false` na-eweghachi nhọrọ nkịtị.                                                 |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(edobeghị)_  | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Ndepụta ndị anabatara maka ebe selector-control nke local-core na-elekwasị anya gafee loopback (aha host ma ọ bụ IP ndị e kewara site na rikoma/ohere, `*` na-emepe LAN/private mana ọ naghị emeghe metadata/link-local ma ọlị). A na-anabata loopback site na http/https na ndabara. Uru DB nke dashboard ga-emeri mgbanwe a mgbe edobere ya ma ọ bụghị ihe efu.                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`      | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke idowe 429 n'akụkụ, nke na-amụba okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọ mere ọzọ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. A kpaara ya n'etiti `1000..3600000`; uru gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara tinyere ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`      | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oke kachasị (ms) nke idowe 429 n'akụkụ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. A kpaara ya n'etiti `1000..3600000`; uru gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara tinyere ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe oke kachasị dị ala karịa ntọala, ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara. Ibelata oke kachasị na-emekwa ka ichefu usoro ahụ dị mkpụmkpụ: a na-ehichapụ ndenye `2 x max` mgbe oge ha gwụsịrị.                                                     |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`       | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ a ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala DB nke dashboard ga-emeri ya). Maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ nke a na-ekewa oke ha site na adreesị ọpụpụ, debe onye otu ọdọ nke na-ekerịta adreesị ọpụpụ achọpụtara nke onye otu a jụrụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya ozugbo n'okpuru ndị otu dị mma. Ọ bụ naanị nhazi; anaghị ewepụ ya ma ọlị. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbaàmà ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`      | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Window rebound (ms) nke na-amachibido ọkwa webhook `proxy.set_aside` ugboro ugboro maka onye otu ọdọ ọ bụla, ka ọtụtụ ọjụjụ n'otu ntabi anya ziga naanị otu mmemme. A na-agụ ya mgbe ọ bụla a na-ezipụ ya. A kpaara ya n'etiti `1000..3600000`; uru gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara n'enweghị ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                         |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(edobeghị)_  | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Onye nnọchi anya HTTP maka oku ndị na-aga n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(edobeghị)_  | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Onye nnọchi anya HTTPS maka oku ndị na-aga n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(edobeghị)_  | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Onye nnọchi anya zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edoghị ya)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-eme ka ebumnuche echo-IP nke nyocha ọpụpụ proxy jiri naanị otu URL. Mgbe edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-anwale `api64.ipify.org`, mesịa `api4.ipify.org`, ka a ghara ịkọ na tunnel ndị na-akwado naanị IPv4 anaghị arụ ọrụ (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edoghị ya)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Aha nnabata/IP ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke a ga-agafeghị na proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ socket kachasị elu nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge maka dispatcher proxy HTTP/SOCKS ọ bụla echekwara na cache. Stream SSE ndị na-adịte aka dịka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ njikọ karịrị otu mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-eji otu proxy nke ọkwa akaụntụ. A na-amachi uru karịrị `256`.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`       | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Oge nkwụsị handshake (njikọ) SOCKS5 na ms. Bulie ya mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-erute otu host gateway nke ụlọ n'otu oge (dịka ọmụmaatụ, arịrịọ 100 n'otu oge) — handshake n'ezie nwere ike ịgafe 10s mgbe pool jupụtara, ọbụlagodi na proxy ahụ ka dị, nke nwere ike ime ka ozi ụgha `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` pụta. A na-amachi ya na `120000`.                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`       | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), a na-**ajụ arịrịọ (fail-closed)** ma ọ bụrụ na proxy e kenyere ya enweghị ike idozi kama ịlaghachi na njikọ kpọmkwem — nke a na-egbochi mkpughe real-IP. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachite DIRECT fallback ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`       | `open-sse/executors`                           | Jiri wreq-js mee ka fingerprint TLS yie nke ọzọ (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Ọ na-egbochi mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edoghị ya)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Allowlist provider ndị e ji rikoma kewaa maka routing TLS ọhụrụ nke na-agafe proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS kpọmkwem na-ejigide omume ochie ya mgbe edoghị ya; naanị provider ndị a ka a na-eziga site na bridge fingerprint Chrome-124.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`       | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka context browser Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie asambodo HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Ọnọdụ ojiji

| Ọnọdụ                                                      | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site na tunnel SSH**                              | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy HTTP ụlọ ọrụ**                                     | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi fingerprint**                                    | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (etinyelarị ya)                                                           |
| **Ọpụpụ a na-achịkwa / enweghị nnweta kpọmkwem**           | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrịọ na-ada kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị kama izipụta ozi site na njikọ kpọmkwem. |
| **Usoro ochie / mmepe — kwe ka fallback kpọmkwem rụọ ọrụ** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachite omume tupu e mesie nchekwa ike: a na-eji njikọ kpọmkwem mgbe mkpebi proxy dara.    |

> **Rịba ama (ịgafe nkwado NVIDIA — #3226):** Endpoint nkwado API-key nke NVIDIA
> na-akwụsị mgbe e zigara ya site na fetch zuru ụwa ọnụ nke proxy/TLS gbanwere (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` na-ama ụma agafe
> patch proxy maka naanị otu oku nkwado ahụ site na iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ ewepu edekọtara nke nwere oke a kapịrị ọnụ — ọ **dịghị** emetụta ọpụpụ chat/usage.
> `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-eme ka oke nke bypass ahụ ghara ịgbanwe.

---

## 9. Njikọ Ngwaọrụ CLI

Na-achịkwa otu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = chọọ n'ime PATH sistemụ; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ ndị akọwapụtara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(edoghị)_                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ntinye PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọta binary CLI (e ji akara kọlọn kewaa ha).                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(edoghị)_                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Dochie ndekọ ụlọ eji agụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị ụzọ zuru ezu nke dị n'ime ụlọ prosesụ ahụ — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e ji bind-mount tinye (ọ bụ otu a ka `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-alaghachi na ndekọ ụlọ. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (ime token ka ọ dị ọhụrụ, data session). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka mbọ ọ bụla ide nhazi CLI daa site na njehie doro anya nke sị "agbanyụrụ ide ihe".                                                               |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, emesịa `cursor`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary agent Cursor. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, nchọpụta ga-ebu ụzọ nwalee `agent`, wee laghachi na `cursor` ma ọ bụrụ na nke ahụ arụghị ọrụ.                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Mgbanwe ga-eji usoro `x.y.z`; uru na-ezighi ezi ga-alaghachi na ndabara a kwadoro.                                                                                                                                               |
| `DEVIN_DESKTOP_EXTENSION_VERSION` | `1.48.2`                         | `open-sse/executors/devin-desktop.ts`               | `extension_version` nke Codeium/language-server e jikọtara, nke dị iche na `ide_version` nke Desktop. Mgbanwe ga-eji `x.y.z`; uru na-ezighi ezi ga-eji ndabara e jikọtara.                                                                                        |
| `CLI_DEVIN_AGENTIC_BIN`           | `devin`                          | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli-agentic.ts`           | Mgbanwe Devin CLI maka àkwà mmiri agentic naanị. Onye mmebe ahụ na-anabata naanị ACP stdio upstream nke mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DEVIN_AGENTIC_HOME`              | _(achọrọ)_                       | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli-agentic.ts`           | Ebe obibi zuru oke e kewapụrụ iche maka subprocess Devin agentic; ụzọ àkwà mmiri a na-anabata bụ `/home/bridge` na ụzọ `.sandbox` nke ọrụ mpaghara (na Windows, `C:\...\.sandbox\...`).                                                                           |
| `DEVIN_AGENTIC_ACP_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `120000`                         | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli-agentic.ts`           | Oge kachasị ogologo maka otu ntụgharị Devin ACP tupu àkwà mmiri ahụ akwụsị child ma weghachi njehie ngafe oge doro anya.                                                                                                                                          |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`              | `devin-cli-agentic/swe-1-7`      | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                   | Aha ọzọ nke ụdị Claude Code bụ isi maka àkwà mmiri e kewapụrụ iche. Live harness na-eji ụdị akaụntụ Devin dị ugbu a weghachiri dochie ihe atụ ahụ.                                                                                                                |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_SONNET_MODEL`       | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`             | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                   | Aha ọzọ nke àkwà mmiri e kewapụrụ iche a na-eji mgbe Claude Code rịọrọ ndabara Sonnet ya.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_OPUS_MODEL`         | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`             | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                   | Aha ọzọ nke àkwà mmiri e kewapụrụ iche a na-eji mgbe Claude Code rịọrọ ndabara Opus ya.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_HAIKU_MODEL`        | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`             | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                   | Aha ọzọ nke àkwà mmiri e kewapụrụ iche a na-eji mgbe Claude Code rịọrọ ndabara Haiku ya.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`     | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`             | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                   | Aha ọzọ nke àkwà mmiri e kewapụrụ iche a na-eji maka ndị enyemaka nọ n'okpuru Claude Code.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DEVIN_SEAT_API_URL`              | `https://server.codeium.com`     | `open-sse/services/usage/devinCli.ts`               | Nhọrọ iji dochie API njikwa oche Codeium nke oke Devin CLI (`GetUserStatus`) na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `AUGGIE_BIN`                      | `auggie`                         | `open-sse/executors/auggie.ts`                      | Nhọrọ iji ụzọ zuru ezu dochie binary CLI nke Augment (Auggie) nke onye na-eweta `auggie` mpaghara na-eji. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọ ga-eji `CLI_AUGGIE_BIN`, mesịa chọọ na PATH.                                                                                       |
| `CLI_AUGGIE_BIN`                  | `auggie`                         | `open-sse/executors/auggie.ts`                      | Nhọrọ aha ọzọ iji dochie ụzọ binary CLI nke Augment (Auggie) (a na-enyocha ya mgbe `AUGGIE_BIN` gasịrị).                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ZCODE_BIN`                       | `zcode`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Binary nke onye ahịa stdio nke onye na-eweta `zcode` mpaghara na-eji. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọ ga-eji `zcode` dị na PATH.                                                                                                                                             |
| `ZCODE_ARGS`                      | —                                | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Ndepụta JSON (eriri ≤16) nke arụmụka ndị ọzọ a na-eziga na binary `zcode` mgbe e ji `cliTools` malite ya.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ZCODE_CWD`                       | `process.cwd()`                  | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Ndekọ ọrụ maka subprocess app-server nke ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ZCODE_PROVIDER_ID`               | `builtin:zai-coding-plan`        | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Nhọrọ iji dochie ID onye na-eweta a na-eziga na app-server.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_RUNTIME_ROOT`       | `~/.zcode/server`                | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Mgbọrọgwụ runtime app-server nke ZCode (ebe `node` na `zcode-server.cjs` ndị e jikọtara ọnụ dị).                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_NODE`               | `<runtimeRoot>/node`             | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Ngwa Node a na-eji kwado app-server nke ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_ENTRY`              | `<runtimeRoot>/zcode-server.cjs` | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Skript mbata app-server a na-eji kwado sava ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ZCODE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `10000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Oge njedebe mmalite (ms) tupu e were na mmalite app-server nke ZCode adaala.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ZCODE_RPC_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `30000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Oge njedebe RPC maka arịrịọ ọ bụla (ms) maka oku app-server nke ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ZCODE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `120000`                         | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Oge kachasị ogologo (ms) nke otu ntụgharị ZCode tupu onye nlekọta kwụsị ya n'ihi oge agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                            | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Nkeji etiti nyocha ugboro ugboro (ms) maka mmecha ntụgharị ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                      | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts` | Ndekọ ụlọ Hermes Agent ebe OmniRoute na-agụ/edegara nhazi Hermes CLI. Ọ dabara na env var nke installer Hermes PowerShell na-edobe na Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                          |

### Mmekọrịta Akpaka nke Profaịlụ CLI

Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a bụ nke a ga-eji aka họrọ ma agbanyụrụ ha na ndabara. A pụkwara ịgbanwu ha site na
dashboard CLI Code.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta model onye na-eweta gasịrị, ọ na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ugbu a. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Codex na-arụ ọrụ/nke ndabara, auth, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-eweta.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta model onye na-eweta gasịrị, ọ na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` na akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ugbu a. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Claude na-arụ ọrụ/nke ndabara, auth, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-eweta. |

### Ọmụmaatụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binary ndị dị na host n'ime container ma gwa OmniRoute ebe ha dị:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e mere bind-mount n'ezie nke ụzọ ahụ site na
host — jikọta ya na mounts dịka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee
profaịlụ `host` dị na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ na-adịghị n'ime
home nke onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ bind mount anya, n’ihi na ihe e dere ebe ahụ ga-efunahụ
mgbe e megharịrị container ahụ.

Image ahụ na-arụ ọrụ dịka `USER node`, ya mere `/root` a na-emeghị mount bụ **ọ bụghị** ezigbo
override.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Nke ndabara     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaghị aka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Manye nchọpụta container ka ọ dị ọkụ (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). A chọrọ ya naanị na runtimes nke nchọpụta akpaka anaghị achọpụta. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka e dee nhazi CLI-tool n’ụzọ container a na-emeghị mount n’agbanyeghị nke ahụ. Ihe ya na ya hà na CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.           |

### Ndị enyemaka CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Mgbanwe ndị a na-ahazi omume nke CLI binary `omniroute` n’onwe ya (ọ bụghị nchọpụta
sidecar dị n’elu).

| Mgbanwe                               | Nke ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemụ)_ | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Manye asụsụ mmepụta CLI. Locale BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-aka env vars locale sistemụ (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ stdout/stderr sava na terminal n’ọnọdụ nlekọta. Ya na flag `--log` na `omniroute serve` hà.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth a na-etinye dịka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka na ọrụ 8.12.                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Mgbọrọgwụ home Gemini na-adịgide adịgide; ọsọ otu-ugboro na-eji home nwa oge e kewapụrụ iche.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`      | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge ebere na milliseconds tupu e jiri ike kwụsị CLI child e zigara signal.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ 1 tinyere DATA_DIR akọwapụtara iji mafere faịlụ environment data-directory ndabara nke onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`     | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Oge ngwụcha HTTP kwa nnwale (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → sava.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`     | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI na-eche endpoint ahụike sava tupu o bipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị oge ngwụcha. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite oyi na-adịghị ngwa (dịka Windows). A pụkwara ịtọ ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`         | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr n’oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory omenala maka nchọpụta plugin CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*`). Mgbe edoghị ya, ndabara bụ `~/.omniroute/plugins/`. Naanị CLI — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-erute scanner plugin nke akụkụ sava, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-atụ aka na ya (ngalaba 2). |
