## 10. Njikọ Agent Dị N'ime & MCP

| Agbanwe                                         | Uru Ndabara                                         | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | nchọpụta-akpaka                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL akọwapụtara nke ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji ruo OmniRoute. Ọ na-akagbu nchọpụta-akpaka localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(edobeghị)_                                        | Modul MCP/A2A                                               | Igodo API maka oku ngwaọrụ MCP dị n'ime na oku nka A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(edobeghị)_                                        | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | NJ ID igodo maka ikenye ihe ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(edobeghị)_                                        | Nke ochie                                                   | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ọnụọgụ ụbọchị a ga-edowe akụkọ ọrụ A2A na ọdụ data mpaghara tupu nhichapụ kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ na ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ ga-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Ihe nkwụsị-ngwa-ngwa maka njirimara nlele memory-hits nke A2A. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji kwụsị kpamkpam nchọgharị ncheta maka ọrụ; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere mgbe edobeghị ya) ga-ahapụ ya ka ọ na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-eme ka endpoint nhazi mbụ edekọtara nke Issue Agent na-adịghị n'ịntanetị/mpaghara rụọ ọrụ. Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ belụsọ mgbe a na-agba usoro ọrụ nhazi mbụ edekọtara na mpaghara n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(edobeghị)_                                        | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka otu ịgba ọsọ nhazi mbụ edekọtara nke Issue Agent. A na-amachi ya ruo n'ogo kachasị dị n'ime; ọ na-alaghachi na uru ndabara arụnyere mgbe edobeghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ na-ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(context na-arụ ọrụ)_                              | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Context/profaịlụ ọnọdụ-remote nke CLI maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-akagbu context na-arụ ọrụ dị n'ụlọ nchekwa context mpaghara. Ya na `--context <name>` bụ otu ihe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ backend keychain OS `keytar` nhọrọ maka ozi nzere context CLI. Mgbe agbanyere nke a, ozi nzere ga-anọgide na ọnọdụ `0600` nke `config.json`, CLI ga-ewepụtakwa ịdọ aka ná ntị ọdịda-nkwụghachi otu ugboro; e zubere ya maka ọrụ headless/container a kpachaara anya họrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa nnweta dabere na scope n'oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(niile)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scope ndị e ji rikoma kewaa: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Kpakọta nkọwa ngwaọrụ MCP tupu e mee serializing manifest ahụ. Uru ndị na-eme ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algọridim/profaịlụ mkpakọ. Uru ndị na-eme ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ọgụgụ njikwa dị n'ime sava MCP (ahụike, nkwụsi ike, combos, oke ojiji, ojiji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke nchere tupu nkwụsị (ms) maka ngafe MCP ndị na-echere onye na-eweta ọrụ (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke ọnọdụ ndeksi corpus mpaghara echekwara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu maka ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ ọ bụla e mere ndeksi). A na-amanye ya ka ọ ghara ịdị ala karịa `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `24`                                                | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model, n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Ogologo oge n'etiti nyocha oke ọnụego na oke ojiji nke onye na-eweta ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere oge (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke OAuth na-esochi ibe ha n'oge mmekọrịta buru ibu; a na-enweta njikọ OAuth otu n'otu iji zere iziga ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge nye ọrụ dị n'elu. `0` na-ewepụ nke a (na-eme ha n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Ogologo oge kacha nta (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke dị n'elu na-esochi ibe ha n'ụzọ nyocha tupu arịrịọ ọ bụla/nlekota; ọ na-ekewa oku netwọkụ ndị na-eme n'otu oge ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ dị n'otu IP ghara iziga ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge nye ọrụ dị n'elu. Ejikọrọ ya na ndị na-eweta nnweta oke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (ebe nnweta abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ nkesa izugbe `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/wdg.) akabeghị n'okpuru ya — a na-enyocha ya iche. Ọ naghị emetụta data sitere na cache. `0` na-agbanyụ ya; a na-amanye ya ịnọ n'etiti `0..5000`. |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge nchere (ms) tupu emelite oke onye na-eweta ọrụ mgbe ihe omume ojiji n'ezie gasịrị, iji nye API oke dị n'elu oge idekọ ojiji ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | agbanyere                                           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ site na nnọkọ Chrome CDP metụtara akaụntụ. Ọ na-eji SQLite akwado ebe nchekwa dị na memori ka ngọngọ wee dịgide mgbe LRU wepụrụ ya, mgbe TTL gwụsịrị, mgbe e bidogharịrị sistemụ, ma ọ bụ mgbe arịrịọ iweghachite rutere na instansị ọzọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji debe ngọngọ naanị na memori. Ngọngọ karịrị 512KB na runtime igwe ojii ga-anọgide na-abụ naanị nke memori n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Ọ na-achịkwa ma ọkachamara nhazi ga-apụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Uru na-abụghị efu na-eme ka ngafe ahụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Njikọ OAuth CLI (Nke Ime)

| Mgbanwe             | Ndabara              | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite               | Nkọwa                                      |
| ------------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta-akpaghị aka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL sava maka njikọ nkwenye CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(edoghị)_           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nkwenye maka njikọ CLI.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`                | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | NJ onye ọrụ maka nnọkọ njikọ CLI.          |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(edoghị)_           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.     |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(edoghị)_           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.      |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(edoghị)_           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.    |

## 11. OAuth Provider Credentials

Asambodo arụnyere n'ime maka **mmepe localhost**. Maka ntinye dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nke gị na njikwa onye nrụpụta nke onye na-enye ọrụ ọ bụla.

| Variable                                    | Provider                | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :------------------------------------------ | :---------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Onye ahịa ọha — ọ dịghị ihe nzuzo achọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Gbanwee URI ntụgharị. Nke ndabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)        | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID onye ahịa Muse CLI device-flow ọha. Hapụ ka ọ ghara ịtọlite iji jiri onye ahịa ọha arụnyere n'ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID onye ahịa OAuth nke GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara ọha nke `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` mgbe etinyeghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID ntinye onye ahịa GitHub Copilot ezigara na `Copilot-Integration-Id` na `Editor-Plugin-Version` headers. Nke ndabara bụ `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Igodo API ndabara ejiri site na `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` mgbe enweghị asambodo njikọ ọ bụla dị. Nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Ụzọ omenala gaa na Devin CLI binary (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-edozi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID onye ahịa OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` na URI ntụgharị `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Nlaghachi ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                   | Hazie ka ịgbanwuo Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                   | Nlaghachi igodo API ozugbo (na-agbagha OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                   | ID oghere ọrụ maka Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                   | Aha ọzọ maka `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                   | Gbanwee ndekọ nhazi Qoder CLI (nnọkọ PAT dịpụrụ adịpụ, na-ezere imebi nbanye ihe nchọgharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web            | Akara `tk` n'ihu ihu iji zipụ dị ka `validated` na `/api/chat`. Achọrọ mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ijikọ akara; ma ọ bụghị ya, OmniRoute na-alaghachi na UUID na-enweghị usoro. Lee nsogbu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge guardrail | URL isi dakọtara na OpenAI maka oku nkwụnye ụzọ ọhụụ na-abụghị Anthropic. Na-agbanwe na gburugburu URL OpenAI ochie ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Tụnye na `/v1` nke OmniRoute ma ọ bụ ebe njedebe ọ bụla dakọtara na OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Nsogbu #2232. Mgbe URL bụ `/v1` nke OmniRoute, arịrịọ sub-nkọwa na-eziga `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` ma na-enyocha ya na asambodo njikọ onwe ya edoziri (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ mpaghara, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) ka ntinye `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` wee rụọ ọrụ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge guardrail | Igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Na-agbanwe gburugburu OpenAI / Google gburugburu maka oku nkwụnye ụzọ ọhụụ na-abụghị Anthropic. Ụdị Anthropic na-edebe ụzọ igodo Anthropic raara onwe ha nye. Nsogbu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge guardrail | Ogologo oge a na-echekwa nsonaazụ ụzọ "enweghị onye ruru eru" na cache, na ms (ndabara `30000`). Uru na-ezighi ezi ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-alaghachi na ndabara; `0` na-agbanyụ cache na-adịghị mma. Isi mmalite: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Mepụta ID Onye Ahịa OAuth 2.0 (ụdị: "Ngwa Weebụ")
> 3. Tinye URL ihe nkesa gị dị ka URI ntụgharị ikike
> 4. Dochie ụkpụrụ asambodo na `.env`.

---

## 12. Mgbanwe User-Agent nke Provider

Gbanwee header `User-Agent` a na-ezigara provider ọ bụla dị n'elu. Klas ntọala executor na-ekpebi nke a n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe n'oge runtime:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Variable | Uru Ndabara | Mgbe a ga-emelite ya |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.258 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true` iji zipụ aha tool nke harness ndị ọzọ kpọmkwem na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ ahụ na-aga Anthropic (OAuth nke mbụ na CLIProxyAPI). Na ndabara, executor na-enye aha ọzọ n'ụzọ a kapịrị ọnụ nye aha tool ndị na-abụghị nke Claude Code (Claude Code canonical mapping ebe otu dị, ma ọ bụghị ya PascalCase), ma gbanwee ha azụ na response site na `_toolNameMap`, ka a ghara ịjụ harness nwere tool ndị dị na snake_case dịka third-party client ndị amata site na fingerprint. Maka debugging naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI melitere Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Gbanwee ụdị Codex client n'adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.258` | Gbanwee ụdị Claude Code a na-egosi n'adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic na-amachibido ụfọdụ model dabere na uru a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Gbanwee suffix mkpụrụedemede atọ OmniRoute na-agbakwunye na `cc_version=` n'ime Claude billing block. Bulie ya tinyere `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — ịkpọgide naanị version ahụ na-egosi otu ụzọ `version.revision` nke binary ọ bụla n'ezie anaghị ewepụta. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.81-6` | Gbanwee ụdị Copilot CLI a na-egosi n'adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat melitere |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Mgbe Antigravity IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Gbanwee `clientId` (public id) nke Kiro social device-code |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nhọrọ ịbanye: nyocha CRC zuru ezu nke frame ọ bụla na Kiro event stream (maka debugging stream mebiri emebi) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI melitere |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor melitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye mgbanwe User-Agent maka provider **ọ bụla** site n'iji usoro `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor na-emepụta aha env var ahụ n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe.
