## 13. Ndakọrịta Akara-Mkpịsịaka CLI

Mgbe agbanyere ya, OmniRoute na-ahazigharị isiokwu HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ha kwekọọ kpọmkwem na akara njirimara nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ihe ize ndụ nke ịka akaụntụ gị akara ma na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ Ọ Bụla

| Mgbanwe                  | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                              |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cline          |

### Nke Izugbe

| Mgbanwe          | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Gbaa ndakọrịta akara-mkpịsịaka maka ndị na-enye ọrụ **niile** otu mgbe. |

### Mgbanwe njirimara Kimi Coding CLI

| Mgbanwe                 | Nke ndabara          | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                        | Nkọwa                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ụdị Kimi CLI a na-eziga n'oge oku OAuth/API.              |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(ndabara e jidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ID ngwaọrụ Kimi e jidere nke a na-eji n'isiokwu klayenti. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụkọ ọrụ na mgbanwe User-Agent (§12). Sistemụ akara-mkpịsịaka na-ahụ maka nhazi isiokwu na nhazi mpaghara ahụ, ebe mgbanwe User-Agent na-ahụ maka eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'adabereghị onwe ha.

---

## 14. Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ Igodo API

Igodo API maka ndị na-enye ọrụ na-eji nyocha njirimara ozugbo. **Nhazi akwadoro:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Ịtọ ha site na mgbanwe gburugburu bụ ụzọ ọzọ maka mbugharị Docker ma ọ bụ mbugharị na-enweghị ihu njikwa.

Ụkpụrụ a na-amata: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mgbanwe            | Onye na-enye ọrụ                                     |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                             |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                           |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader ndabere)            |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (aha ọzọ maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)             |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + ndabere nkata |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (aha ọzọ maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)               |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` kwụ ọtọ maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na runtime anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ ahụ na-adabere naanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Nnyocha: Mgbanwe E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghị Arụ Ọrụ_ dị na njedebe akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ mbugharị.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard n'ụdị ezoro ezo na SQLite, ha na-ebutekwa mgbanwe gburugburu ụzọ.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, na `jina-search` anaghị akwụ ụgwọ igodo env klọsta mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` a na-ekekọrịta) dị — `getProviderCredentials` na-ebu ụzọ mejupụta. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị enye `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (aha ọzọ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jiri njikọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo data multimodal nke izizi na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — ihe N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ vector N.

---

## 15. Ntọala Oge Nchere

Uru niile dị na **milisekọnd**. Mkpebi etinyere n'otu ebe na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Usoro Ọkwa Oge Nchere

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (mgbanwe zuru ụwa ọnụ)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (ọkpụkpọ ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n'elu, ndabara: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ, nke a tụrụ aro: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 1260000; 0 na-agbanyụ ya)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (na-amachi mgbatị oge njikere na-agbanwe agbanwe, ndabara: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (e sitere na ya nweta, ndabara: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ)
```

| Mgbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — na-akagbu ndabara nke `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` na `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Ngụkọta oge njedebe arịrịọ HTTP maka oku a na-akpọ ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Ogologo oge nkịtị kacha elu n'etiti raw bytes sitere n'elu tupu a kwụsị ya. Ụdịdị extended-thinking anaghị akwụsịkarị ihe karịrị 90s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Ogologo ndụ kacha elu nke iyi SSE na-arụ ọrụ; ọ naghị amalitegharị mgbe bytes si n'elu bịara ma ọ dabereghị na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. E si na `timeoutMs` kacha ukwuu nke ụdịdị ọ bụla dị na ndekọ ahụ (1200000, Codex) gbakwụnye oke 60000 nweta ya, ka a ghara ịkwụsị ụdịdị e nyere ohere iji mmefu oge ya niile n'etiti azịza. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(agbanyụrụ)_                                                     | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike izipu ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka heartbeat `: keepalive` na trailers metadata `x-omniroute-*`). Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara (#10524) n'ihi na clients dakọtara kpamkpam na OpenAI na-eji JSON.parse ahịrị SSE ọ bụla ma daa mgbe ha hụrụ nkọwa `:`; nke a anaghị emetụta heartbeats `data:`. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` iji họrọ ịbanye ọzọ. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge a na-echere iji nata event SSE mbụ na-abụghị ping. Ọ na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Oge njikere kacha elu nke na-agbanwe agbanwe maka event mbụ n'arịrịọ streaming buru ibu, ndị na-eji ọtụtụ ngwaọrụ, ma ọ bụ ndị chọrọ echiche dị elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Kill-switch maka heuristic `/goal`. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọpụta kpamkpam — a naghị ebuli oge njedebe njikere na mgbake iyi site na request body/headers, nke na-ebelata mmụba oge njedebe nke client na-achịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Oge njikere kacha elu maka event mbụ n'ọrụ agent `/goal` achọpụtara ma ọ bụ arịrịọ e ji `x-omniroute-agent-goal` manye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Mee ka mgbake iyi n'oge dị ngwa rụọ ọrụ na-akpaghị aka maka ọrụ agent `/goal` achọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nhọrọ pụrụ iche nke goal ahụ. Nke a nwere ike naanị ỊGBỤKWUNYE mgbake n'elu ndabara onye nchịkwa — ọ naghị akagbu nhọpụta ịpụ doro anya nke `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/ntọala DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ events SSE `codex.*` na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-eme ka `responses.stream()` nke OpenAI SDK daa na 502. Agbanyere ya na ndabara (#11014). Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` iji zipụ ha n'ihu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Transport Codex app-server a na-ahọrọ ịbanye na ya. Endpoint WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke sidecar `codex app-server` dị na mpaghara. Mgbe edobere ya na token, a na-ebufe arịrịọ Codex site na JSON-RPC gaa na sidecar kama iji HTTP Responses API. A pụkwara ịtọ ya maka njikọ ọ bụla site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Token ikike/bearer inline a na-enye app-server. Ndochi maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Path gaa na faịlụ nwere token ikike app-server (sitere na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`. Ndochi maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Directory ọrụ ebe ntụgharị app-server na-arụ ọrụ. Ndochi maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Iwu nkwado a na-enyefe ntụgharị app-server (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Ndochi maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Iwu sandbox a na-enyefe ntụgharị app-server (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe edoghị ya, executor na-eji `workspace-write` dịka ndabara (e wusiri nchekwa ya ike; ọ bụbu `danger-full-access`). Ndochi maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Kwado arịrịọ nkwado nke app-server n'onwe ya na-akpaghị aka (mmezu command/faịlụ/ikike na host). Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — a na-ajụ arịrịọ ndị ahụ na-akpaghị aka; nke a anaghị emetụta oku ngwaọrụ harness (ha na-agafe n'ụzọ passthrough `item/tool/call` dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Ndochi maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge a na-echere iji nata headers nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Oge kachasị elu a ga-echere nkụnye isi nzaghachi maka arịrịọ nkwanye data (ms). `0` na-ewepụ oke a; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` nke onye na-eweta ọrụ kọwara kpọmkwem nwere ikike karịa ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Oge kachasị elu a ga-echere ka nzaghachi malite (ms) maka nnwale ọ bụla kpọmkwem na-enweghị proxy. Mgbe oge gwụrụ, a na-anwale ya ọzọ otu ugboro na socket ọhụrụ; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji wepụ oke ahụ ma debe omume mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (5 min)                                                  | Oge kacha nta a ga-echere ka nzaghachi malite (ms) maka nnwale kpọmkwem na-enweghị proxy nke host ebumnuche ya bụ nke nzuzo/LAN/loopback (dịka ọmụmaatụ `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), ka mmalite oyi nke Ollama nke obodo ghara ịkwụsị n'ihi ndabara 30s dị n'elu. Ọ na-arụ ọrụ dịka oke kacha nta: oge nchere dị irè bụ nke ka ukwuu n'etiti uru a na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị irè na-ewepụ oke kacha nta nke obodo. Nke a anaghị emetụta host ndị dị anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Oke kachasị elu (ms) maka nnwale RETRY nke socket ọhụrụ mgbe oge nchere mmalite nzaghachi nke nnwale e jikọtara ọnụ dị n'elu gwụrụ (#13703). Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe onye kpọrọ ọrụ tinyelarị akara njedebe oge nke ya (usoro njikọ/model/onye na-eweta ọrụ/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` e kpebiri); akara ahụ bụ ezigbo oke, ọ bụkwa ya na-arụ ọrụ mbụ n'ụzọ e zubere, ya mere nke a bụ nkwado sara mbara kama ịbụ oke kwụ ọtọ — ma e wepụ ya, nnwale ọzọ ahụ ga-eji otu obere windo `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` ahụ nnwale e jikọtara ọnụ jiri, ma mee ka model echiche ndị dị mma nwere TTFB dị nwayọ nweta 504. A naghị ekwe ka ọ dị ala karịa oke kacha nta kwụ ọtọ dị n'elu; mgbe onye kpọrọ ọrụ enyeghị akara njedebe oge ọ bụla, nnwale ọzọ ahụ na-edebe oke kacha nta kwụ ọtọ ahụ n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nata body nzaghachi zuru ezu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha maka ịmepụta njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Oge ngwụcha mgbe socket keep-alive anaghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge ngwụcha proxy akara mkpịsị aka TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Na-amachi kpọmkwem oge a ga-ewe tupu byte mbụ erute na body nke nnyefe akara mkpịsị aka TLS nke wreq-js; naanị `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` enweghị ike ịchọpụta body kwụsịrị n'ihi na ọ na-emecha ozugbo nkụnye isi rutere (#12656). Mgbe oge gwụrụ, ọ na-akagbu onye na-agụ wreq ma laghachi na dispatcher kpọmkwem/proxy; `0` na-ewepụ watchdog ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị, nakwa naanị mgbe ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị ọkụ (ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara): na-amachi oge nchere maka byte mbụ nke body nke nzaghachi Responses a na-ebunye mgbe nkụnye isi ya rutere (#13484). Stream Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị gafee windo a bụ nkwụsị: a na-eme ka akaụntụ ahụ jụọ oyi ma bugharịa arịrịọ ahụ gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ugboro; nkwụsị nke abụọ na-eme ka ọ daa ozugbo. `0` na-ewepụ nche ahụ ọbụna mgbe ọkọlọtọ ahụ dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= agbanyụrụ)                                                 | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: na-amachi oge nchere maka nkụnye isi nzaghachi upstream na oku Responses a na-ebunye mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ ka dị — arịrịọ nọ n'ahịrị na-akwụsị ichere ma gafee kama ijide windo nkụnye isi niile. Ọ na-emetụta naanị oku Responses a na-ebunye (stream Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị n'ebe ahụ bụ ahịrị nchere, ọ bụghị mmepụta); emetụghị mmecha mkparịta ụka na oku ndị anaghị ebunye, windo ahụ na-ebelata naanị oke fetch-start dị irè, ọ naghị agbatị ya. Uru a tụrụ aro mgbe a na-eme ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ: `30000`. `0` na-ewepụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: ugboro ole n'otu arịrịọ oke nchere nkụnye isi dị n'elu nwere ike ịkwaga na akaụntụ ọzọ. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-edebe windo nkụnye isi zuru ezu mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: dọba arịrịọ ahụ na heartbeat mgbe 429 na-adịru nwa oge mere ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ mgbe e nwere akara nrụgide pool ọhụrụ), wee megharịa otu akụkụ e nyere oke nke nwere ihe ruru akaụntụ 3 na-esochi onwe ha kama ịgbasa ya n'ìgwè niile (#13924). Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-ebuga ya na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Maka mkparịta ụka nkwanye data naanị: mgbe body upstream mbụ kwụsịrị tupu o wepụta event a pụrụ iji mee ihe, mee otu nnwale nke abụọ e nyere oke site n'otu ụzọ routing ahụ, jiri otu readiness budget ahụ, na-enweghị ntaramahụhụ akaụntụ. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara: body mbụ kwụsịrị na-eme ka arịrịọ ahụ daa n'emeghị nnwale ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: mgbe 429 si n'aka onye na-eweta ọrụ e kewara site na egress n'elu akaụntụ na-enweghị proxy n'okpuru ọnọdụ pool gbara ya gburugburu, rịọ connection pool ka ọ họrọ onye otu ọzọ maka nnwale na-esote kama ịnwale otu adreesị egress ahụ ọzọ. Ọ na-ahazi usoro, ọ naghị ewepụ ihe ọ bụla: pool agwụla ga-edebe omume ugbu a. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-ebuga ya na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(edoghị ya)_                                                     | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: gbanwee path nke akara nrụgide pool a na-agụ tupu ịdọba arịrịọ (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, ndabara `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Akara ọhụrụ na-adọba ya n'agụghị ọnụ ọzọ; ma ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị ma ọ bụ na ọ kara nká, ọ na-alaghachi na burst counter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe nke proxy hop maka arịrịọ bridge `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Tụnye onye mmebe web-fetch nke Firecrawl aka n’instance nke a na-akwado n’onwe gị (igodo API abụghị iwu ma ọ bụrụ na ọ nọghị na cloud).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe maka arịrịọ ọ bụla nke onye mmebe web-fetch nke Firecrawl.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(edoghị)_                                                        | URL ntọala maka onye na-eweta oke `/usage` nke Tavily (Oke Provider); a na-eleghara ya anya mgbe ọ na-arụtụ aka na `api.tavily.com`. | `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Oge iji zipụ response headers site na bridge ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Oge njedebe mgbe njikọ keep-alive nke bridge anaghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Oge njedebe raw socket (0 = agbanyụrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Oge amara mgbe SIGTERM/SIGINT gasịrị tupu mmemme ahụ apụ n’ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Uru ndabere nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe edoghị `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge njedebe (ms) maka preset `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` dị na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Bulie ya maka endpoint ndị na-adịghị ọsọ ọsọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịgbanwegharị n’etiti active/error na dashboard. Ọ na-alaghachi na 8000ms maka uru na-ezighi ezi (<1000) ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Oge njedebe fetch pụrụ iche maka relay dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay kwụsịrị ịzaghachi ga-ada tupu oge njedebe client/agent (~30s) ka ndị kpọrọ ya hụ ọdịda pụrụ iche nke relay kama oge njedebe upstream izugbe. E debere oke ya na `29000` ka ọ na-ebute ụzọ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Oge nchere retry a na-ekekọrịta maka ụzọ direct/relay/proxy ndị na-anwalegharị naanị otu ugboro dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = nwaa ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ native wreq-js (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke hard-deadline JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Jiri nsonaazụ ọchụchọ zaa ajụjụ ozugbo." na ozi system nke onye kpọrọ ya (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara — Perplexity na-eme ọchụchọ n’agbanyeghị nke ahụ, ahịrịokwu ahụ na-apụtakwa na nzaghachi dị ka nkọwa meta nye client ndị na-ede code. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachite ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke hard-deadline JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ native wreq-js (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya ruo `180000` maka arịrịọ ọ bụla mgbe generation na-ewe ogologo oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke hard-deadline JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Pool browser Playwright a na-ekekọrịta maka chat web-cookie nke browser na-akwado (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ `off` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Path gaa na binary `obscura` nke browser pool na onye mmebe Cloudflare Playground na-eji dị ka engine bụ isi (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na PATH nke sistemụ mgbe edoghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(edobeghi ya)_                                                   | Tụnye aka na Obscura na-agba ọsọ ugbua (`http://host:port`) kama ịmalite nke ọhụrụ; modul ahụ anaghị achịkwa proses ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri efu ahọpụtara na-enweghị usoro`                   | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri akọwapụtara maka `obscura serve` a malitere; a na-ahọrọ ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri efu na-akpaghị aka ma ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Mee ka arịrịọ nkata web-cookie jiri ụzọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); jiri `1` mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL ntọala maka ihe mmebe Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC nke mba ụwa) (`kimi-web.ts`); gbanwee ya naanị maka endpoint enyo/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Endpoint nkata zuru ezu maka ihe mmebe Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(achọpụtara na-akpaghị aka)_                                     | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ Chrome/Edge sistemụ a pụrụ ịgba ọsọ maka nbanye ihe nchọgharị mmekọrịta Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ọ na-edochi nchọpụta akpaghị aka nke OS ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _ihe nrụpụta standalone nke .build/_                              | Mgbanwe n'oge build maka ndekọ ihe nrụpụta standalone nke usoro idokọta ihe ọnụ mgbe build gasịrị na-eji (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); nke a bụ maka ngwa build, ọ bụghị oge ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Mgbalị combo target na-eketa oge nchere arịrịọ upstream e kpebiri (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara fetch). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` n'ime combo,
ndabara combo, ma ọ bụ provider override naanị iji mee ka combo fallback dị ngwa; a na-amachi ụkpụrụ karịrị
oge nchere upstream dị ugbu a ruo n'ókè oge nchere upstream ahụ.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu oge wall-clock dị iche maka combo dum gafee target failover niile.
Hapụ ya n'edoghị ya ma ọ bụ tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji nọgide na-eme iteration na-enweghị oke (ihe nkwụsị hardcoded nke nkeji 10 na
`comboPredicates.ts` ka na-emetụta). Uru dị mma na-anọchi
usoro nchekwa ahụ maka combo. Mee ka `comboTimeoutMs` dị ogologo karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ka failover
ka nwee oge mgbe target mbụ jiri nwayọọ.

### Ókè Nkwụsị Circuit Breaker

Nhazi circuit breaker n'ọkwa provider. Ndagburu na-egosipụta ụkpụrụ ndị a gbasawanyere ejirila kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500+.

| Mgbanwe                                                       | Ndabara   | Faịlụ Isi Mbido                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oke ọdịda ndị na-aga n’usoro maka ndị na-eweta OAuth tupu sekit breeka ahụ agbanyụọ.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo nrụgharị (ms) maka breeka onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oke ọdịda ndị na-aga n’usoro maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo nrụgharị (ms) maka breeka onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oke ọdịda ndị na-aga n’usoro maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo nrụgharị (ms) maka breeka onye na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda n’ime windo tupu onye na-eweta OAuth niile abanye n’oge izu ike.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izu ike (ms) mgbe onye na-eweta OAuth ruru oke ahụ.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụọgụ kachasị eji amụba mmụba resetTimeout nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-abawanye azụndọghachi mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda n’ime windo tupu onye na-eweta API-key niile abanye n’oge izu ike.                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izu ike (ms) mgbe onye na-eweta API-key ruru oke ahụ.                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụọgụ kachasị eji amụba mmụba resetTimeout nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta API-key na-abawanye azụndọghachi mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta mpaghara niile abanye n’oge izu ike.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Breeka n’ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izu ike (ms) mgbe onye na-eweta mpaghara ruru oke ahụ.                                                                  |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Omimi azụndọghachi nke a na-ewere onye na-eweta nke ntụtụ context-cache dị na ya dịka nke ahụike ya dara ogologo oge, wee tufuo ntụtụ ahụ maka failover. |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Windo mgbochi ịgbanwegharị ugboro ugboro (ms) nke na-anabata obere oge izu ike na-adịru nwa oge tupu a tufuo ntụtụ context-cache.                        |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                       | Nhazi                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**        | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                    |
| **Kpaa oke ngụkọta oge iyi ahụ ga-adị ndụ** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)             |
| **Ọdịda ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta**        | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                       |
| **Ụdị echiche gbatịrị agbatị**              | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n’etiti iberibe) |
