## 16. Ndekọ

Sistemụ ndekọ na-ede ma na stdout ma na faịlụ ndekọ ndị a na-atụgharịgharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Mgbanwe                                   | Ndabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọdịdị mmepụta: `text` (nke mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri ahazi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ yana stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ụzọ faịlụ ndekọ (n'ihe metụtara mgbọrọgwụ prọjektị ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Ogo faịlụ kachasị tupu ntụgharị. Ọ na-anabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ọnụọgụ ndabere faịlụ ndekọ ndị a tụgharịworo kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ/oku na ebe nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ọnụọgụ ndenye ndekọ oku kachasị na buffer dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `call_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_              | Manye ndekọ arịrịọ zuru ezu ka ọ gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ, na-akagbu ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 elekere)      | Ogologo oge kachasị maka ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ nọ n'ọrụ ma bụrụ nke a hapụrụ tupu nhicha n'ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe stream na pipeline artifacts mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Nhọrọ nke onye ọrụ ga-eme (`true`) — agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji chekwaa ohere diski.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo artifact ndekọ oku pipeline kachasị na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `proxy_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye IP ndị ahịa/egress na prefix akaụntụ na ndekọ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Ihe ndekọ proxy-log nke dashboard/ebe nchekwa data na-edowe nkọwa niile.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji dekọọ `first_chunk_ms` (site na mbido izipu ruo na byte mbụ bara uru nke body) n'ahịrị proxy-log. Ọ na-etinye body upstream ọ bụla n'ime stream passthrough umengwụ ma na-eme patch n'ahịrị ahụ ozugbo byte ahụ rutere; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka response gafee n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 nkeji)          | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyochagharị nha faịlụ ndekọ nọ n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo string kachasị a na-edowe na chat log artifacts (ndabara bụ 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụọgụ ihe array a na-edowe site na ngwụcha mgbe a na-ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi ntinye kachasị tupu ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Object keys kachasị a na-edowe na chat log payloads (0 = enweghị oke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Nha body arịrịọ/response dum (KB) tupu e jiri nchịkọta nkịtị dochie ya kama clone zuru ezu. Welie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka agentic ogologo na-egosi placeholder kama ozi ndị ahụ n'ezie na dashboard.                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe ọ bụ true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-amafere mbipụ dabere na nha. Maka debug naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 17. Ime Ka Ebe Nchekwa Rụọ Ọrụ Nke Ọma

| Ihe mgbanwe                   | Ndabara                                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _akpaka_ (igwe nkịtị); **`1024` n'ime onyonyo Docker** | Oke heap V8 (MB) **akwadoro** maka Docker/ngwa kwụụrụ onwe ya. Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ahazi ya n'ụzọ dinamiki (~35% nke RAM sistemụ, ma debe ya n'agbata `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị oke ala mgbe enweghị ike ịgụ mkpokọta ebe nchekwa. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), a na-agbakwunye uru e nyere **kpọmkwem** dị ka `--max-old-space-size`, ọ bụkwa ya **na-emeri** ọkọlọtọ heap NODE_OPTIONS na-emegide ya (usoro ọkọlọtọ ikpeazụ nke V8). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ ọkọlọtọ heap NODE_OPTIONS dị adị (#5238). Edokwala ha abụọ ka ha nwee ọnụọgụ dị iche — usoro ahụ na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị nke na-akpọpụta uru abụọ ahụ na nke meriri. **Onyonyo Docker gọọmentị na-edobe `1024` mgbe niile, ya mere nhazi akpaka anaghị arụ ọrụ n'ebe ahụ.** Onye nnọchi anya ide koodu `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` tinyere ohere mgbakwunye cgroup — lee [Nduzi Docker — RAM oge ọrụ](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                   | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke ndenye prompt sistemụ echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                       | Ogo mkpokọta cache prompt kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (nkeji 5)                                     | TTL nke ndenye cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                  | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke nzaghachi temperature=0 echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                       | Ogo mkpokọta cache semantic kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                   | TTL nke ndenye cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                    | Mgbọrọgwụ corpus mpaghara ndị na-edobe ndeksi dị ndụ n'ime ebe nchekwa n'otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: mgbe e ruru oke ahụ, a na-ewepụ ndeksi nke mgbọrọgwụ a na-ejighị ogologo oge kachasị, wee wughachi ya mgbe ajụjụ ọzọ gbasara ya bịara. A na-edobe opekempe ya na `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-alaghachi na uru ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                   | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke mmemme stream ọhụrụ dị na buffer nlele dị ndụ nke Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(edoghị)_                                             | Mgbanwe zuru ụwa ọnụ nke ogologo context (token) maka provider ọ bụla na-enweghị ihe mgbanwe `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Mgbe edobere ya, ọ **na-anọchi** catalog, mgbanwe Dashboard na uru registry maka provider ndị ahụ; mgbe edoghị ya, OmniRoute na-eji usoro arụnyere n'ime ya, nke ụzọ ikpeazụ ya bụ `128000`. Lee [Ogologo context nke provider ọ bụla](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                  | Ohere token mgbakwunye edobere mgbe a na-enyocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Ogologo context nke provider ọ bụla (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) na-agụkwa ụdị
**nke provider ọ bụla** nke a na-ewulite aha ya site na ID provider n'oge ọrụ, ya mere enweghị ike
idepụta ya ahịrị n'ahịrị na tebụl dị n'elu ma ọ bụ na `.env.example`.

**Aha.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, ebe `<PROVIDER>` bụ ID provider e dere n'ụdị mkpụrụedemede ukwu, ma jiri
`_` dochie mkpụrụedemede ọ bụla na-abụghị `A-Z` / `0-9`. ID provider ahụ bụ nke canonical mgbe
e mechara mkpebi alias (akụkụ `provider` nke eriri `provider/model` mgbe e mechara dozie alias dị mkpụmkpụ).
Node omenala dakọtara na OpenAI ma ọ bụ Anthropic na-eji ID node e mepụtara nye ha, ya mere
UUID bụ akụkụ nke aha ihe mgbanwe ahụ. Uru ya bụ ọnụọgụ token zuru ezu karịrị efu; ihe ọ bụla
ọzọ (efu, `0`, adịghị mma, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ) ka a na-eleghara anya, mkpebi ahụ wee gaa n'ihu na nzọụkwụ ọzọ.

| NJ onye na-eweta ọrụ                                        | Aha mgbanwe                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Usoro ibute ụzọ.** Maka otu ụzọ `provider/model`, nzọụkwụ mbụ nke wepụtara uru ga-emeri:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` maka onye na-eweta ọrụ ahụ.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (mgbanwe gburugburu zuru ụwa ọnụ).
3. Window nke model ọ bụla: mgbanwe context echekwara (nke edobere na dashboard, ma ọ bụ nke
   discovery reconciler tinyere ma kpọchie na-akpaghị aka, lee `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), ma ọ bụghị ya, uru
   catalog / models.dev e mekọrịtara.
4. `defaultContextLength` nke ndekọ onye na-eweta ọrụ ahụ.
5. Atụmatụ dabere n'aha model (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` /
   `codex` 400000).
6. Ndabara arụnyere n'ime sistemụ maka onye na-eweta ọrụ ọ bụla, emesịa `128000`.

Nzọụkwụ gburugburu abụọ ahụ metụtara **onye na-eweta ọrụ ahụ dum**: model ọ bụla nke onye na-eweta ọrụ ahụ ga-enweta
otu oke ahụ, ha ga-ebutekwa mgbanwe dashboard nke model ọ bụla ụzọ. Mgbe naanị otu model nke onye na-eweta ọrụ
chọrọ window dị iche, jiri mgbanwe nke model ahụ na dashboard kama.

**Oke ọrụ.** Usoro a na-achịkwa nyocha context-window n'oge arịrịọ (ọjụjụ
`context_length_exceeded` / "Ntinye gafere context window" dị na
`open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), oke mmefu mkpakọ prompt na oke context combo. `context_length`
nke `GET /v1/models` na-akpọsa ka nwere ike isi na metadata catalog ma ọ bụ registry
pụta, a naghịkwa ekwe nkwa na ọ ga-egosipụta mgbanwe gburugburu ahụ.

**Ọmụmaatụ.** Onye na-eweta ọrụ nke model ya na-anabata window token 1,050,000 ebe catalog na-akọ
128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Malitegharịa sava ahụ mgbe ị gbanwechara ya: a na-agụ uru ahụ site na gburugburu process sava.
Na **ngwa desktop**, sava ahụ na-arụ ọrụ dị ka child process, ọ naghị ahụkwa system variable
edobere mgbe ebidola ngwa ahụ; tinye ahịrị ahụ na faịlụ `.env` nke ngwa ahụ na-eburu (usoro nchọta dị na
[Ntuziaka Electron: Nchọta faịlụ gburugburu](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)),
emesịa mechie ngwa ahụ kpamkpam ma maliteghachi ya.

### Mkpakọ

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Nkọwa                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | edoghị  | Tụkwasị `.rtk/filters.json` nke project obi na-enweghị hash `.rtk/trust.json`. Jiri naanị na mmepe mpaghara a na-achịkwa. |

### Injin ebe nchekwa (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ event-loop nke ebe nchekwa, skills, na mmelite token (#10349)

OmniRoute bụ **otu process Node**. Mwepụta/nnweta ebe nchekwa, ntinye skills, na mmelite token onye na-eweta ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ n'elu **otu event loop ahụ** dị ka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị worker thread.

| Ọrụ                          | Koodu                              | Ndabara                                                 | Njikwa onye ọrụ sistemụ                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Mwepụta / nnweta ebe nchekwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** nke dashboard (ndabara bụ ịgbanwu ya) | Gbanyụọ **Ntọala → Ebe nchekwa**. E nweghị env kill switch dị iche karịa ịgbanyụọ njirimara ahụ na ntọala.                                 |
| Ntinye skills                | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** nke dashboard (ndabara bụ ịgbanwu ya) | Gbanyụọ **Ntọala → Ebe nchekwa/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Ntọala sandbox ndị dị n'okpuru na-amachi naanị mmezu mgbe agbanwuru ntinye ahụ. |
| Mmelite token                | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Ọ na-arụ ọrụ maka ndị na-eweta OAuth/web ejikọrọ        | Kwụpụ onye na-eweta ọrụ ahụ ma ọ bụ hapụ token ka ha nọgide bụrụ ndị ziri ezi; e nweghị env `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` taa.                         |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` dị nwayọọ n'igbe na-adịghị arụ nnukwu ọrụ, buru ụzọ gbanyụọ ebe nchekwa + skills, emesịa lelee ibu catalog/mkpakọ (#10303, #9685). Njirimara ndị a na-ahapụ ohere n'ebe `await` dị, mana ha ka na-asọrịta mpi maka otu thread ahụ.

Ntọala embedding layer, vector store na reranking maka persistent memory subsystem (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Ihe Mgbanwe                          | Ndabara                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (nkeji 5)          | TTL maka cache embedding dị na ebe nchekwa RAM (maka mbinye aka source/model/dim ọ bụla).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Ọnụọgụ ntinye LRU kachasị elu a na-edobe na cache embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | ID repo HF maka pipeline MiniLM mpaghara `@huggingface/transformers` a na-eji naanị mgbe ahọrọ ya (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | ID repo HF maka embedder static potion/Model2Vec nke na-eji tebụl ọchụchọ. A na-ebudata ya nwayọọ nwayọọ n'ime ndekọ cache mgbe achọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Ndekọ eji echekwa faịlụ model static potion na cache. Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-eji ebe dị n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` dịka ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Dochie URL ntọala Hugging Face Hub nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dịka endpoint mirror maka nhazi air-gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K ndabara nke ọchụchọ vector brute-force `sqlite-vec` dị n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Konstant Reciprocal Rank Fusion `k` maka iweghachite ngwakọta FTS5 + vector (usoro sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Ebe njikọ nnwale/nchọpụta nsogbu na `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amanye vector store ka ọ bụrụ `null` (na-eme ka gburugburu cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec` dị ka eziokwu), na-eme ka iweghachite ebe nchekwa laghachi na ọchụchọ mkpụrụokwu FTS5. Hapụ ya ka edoghị ya na production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(edoghị ya)_               | Igodo API maka backend Notion (`genericBackend.ts` na-eji ya na preset backend amaara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | URL ntọala maka API Notion (enwere ike dochie ya maka nhọrọ Notion a na-akwado n'onwe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(edoghị ya)_               | Igodo API maka backend Obsidian Vault (`genericBackend.ts` na-eji ya na preset backend amaara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | URL ntọala maka API Obsidian Vault (enwere ike dochie ya maka vault dị anya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ire ere ebe nchekwa typed TV6. **A na-eji ya naanị mgbe ahọrọ ya (ndabara bụ gbanyụọ)** — sweep ahụ **na-ehichapụ** ebe nchekwa ndị rere ere. Mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ naanị telemetry, ọ dịghịkwa ihe a ga-ehichapụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) nke ebe nchekwa `episodic` anaghị eji amalite ire ere mgbe ọ gafesịrị. `0` na-emekwa ka episodic ghara ire ere. Ụdị na-adịgide (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) anaghị ere ere mgbe niile. Elekere ire ere na-amalitegharị site na `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ebe nchekwa etinyere ugboro `>=` ọnụ ọgụgụ a agaghịzi ere ere n'agbanyeghị ụdị ya. `0` na-agbanyụ nchebe sitere na nnweta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (agbanyụrụ)             | Oge etiti (sekọnd) maka sweep ire ere oge na-aga nke nhọrọ dị na `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/edoghị ya = enweghị sweep oge na-aga. A ga-ahọrọ ya ugboro abụọ: ọ chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(edoghị ya)_               | ID ndị provider e ji rikoma kewaa (anaghị ele nnukwu/mkpụrụedemede anya) nke na-anabata ozi `system` **naanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, ịtinye ebe nchekwa n'etiti array n'ụzọ na-echekwa cache adịghị nchebe na mkparịta ụka nwere ọtụtụ ntụgharị, ya mere a na-ejikọta/tinye ebe nchekwa n'ihu dịka ozi system mbụ. Ndabara bụ naanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbasaa ya maka endpoint dakọtara na OpenAI a na-akwado n'onwe (dịka Qwen3.5+/3.6) nke template chat ya na-amanye otu mmachi ahụ nke naanị otu ozi system dị n'ihu. |

### Ọmụmaatụ Docker Nwere RAM Dị Ala

`128` bụ naanị maka dashboard. Ndị agent coding nọ na heap a na-enweta `FATAL ERROR` n'oge `/v1/responses` dị ogologo. Ejila ọmụmaatụ a dịka gateway Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| Mgbanwe                 | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta        | Nkọwa                                            |
| ----------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `PRICING_SYNC_ENABLED`  | `false`       | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Mmekọrịta ọnụahịa oge ọbụla a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.              |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Isi mmalite data ndị rikoma kewara.              |

## Mmekọrịta Arena ELO

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Mmekọrịta ELO nke bọọdụ ndị-isi Arena AI n'oge ụfọdụ, nke a pụrụ ịhazi site na Dashboard Feature Flags ma ọ bụ jiri `false` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Ụdị prefix a na-eji maka id ụdịdị na `GET /v1/models`. `dual` na-akpọsa ma prefix alias dị mkpụmkpụ ma prefix provider canonical maka ụdịdị ọ bụla (ndakọrịta azụ — ọ fọrọ nke nta ka ọ bụrụ na ọ na-eme katalọgụ ahụ okpukpu abụọ); `alias` na-ewepụta otu id maka ụdịdị ọ bụla; `canonical` na-ewepụta naanị prefix provider-id zuru ezu (providers nke alias ha bụrịrị id canonical na-edobe otu ndenye ha). Clients nwere ike iji `?prefix=alias` gbanwee ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla. Lee [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## Provider PromptQL Playground (Na-abụghị nke Gọọmentị/Nnwale)

Njikọ nnọkọ GraphQL e ji reverse engineering mepụta maka prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-arụtụ aka na endpoints playground ọha; gbanwee naanị maka deployment PromptQL nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị/ọzọ.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara                                                            | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                       | Nkọwa                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint GraphQL e ji arụ ọrụ nkata/nnọkọ.                     |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | Endpoint GraphQL e ji ajụ banyere itule kredit/ojiji.          |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint e ji anwa ime ka token dị ọhụrụ.                      |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Oge kachasị (ms) iji na-enyocha `thread_events` tupu oge agwụ. |

## Onye Na-eweta Weebụ Adobe Firefly (Nke Na-abụghị nke Gọọmenti/Nnwale)

Mmegharị nnọkọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-achịkwa maka onye na-eweta weebụ Adobe Firefly
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — a haziri ndabara niile maka nrụnye
desktọpụ nkịtị.

> **E wepụrụ ya na #9255.** E ji ọrụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị Playwright dochie sistemụ Chrome ochie e jikọrọ na CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts), ya mere ntọala njikwa ya adịghịzi. Ngbanwe
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tinyere
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS na ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, anaghị agụ ebe ọ bụla n'ime
> codebase — ịtọ ha enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.

| Ngbanwe                              | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` maka Chrome headless n'ezie (a maara na ọ naghị arụ ọrụ maka generate; maka debugging naanị).                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ịhọrọ ịgbanye/gbanyụọ ikpo ihe nchọgharị ọkụ tupu oge eruo. `0` na-akwụsị ikpo ọkụ tupu oge eruo (mgbake 408 n'etiti batch ka na-arụ ọrụ). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji kwụsị ichekwa nnọkọ Adobe Firefly na diski.                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere kacha nta (ms) a na-amanye n'etiti submit ndị na-esochi ibe ha, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere mgbakwunye (ms) a na-etinye mgbe batch gara nke ọma, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) tupu izipu arịrịọ generation, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                       |

## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(edobeghị)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Nchịkwa siri ike maka mmekọrịta ọnụahịa models.dev. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya = soro Ntọala > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **na-emeri DB** ma na-amafere ma mmekọrịta oge niile ma nyocha SQL/JSON nke `getModelsDevPricing()` (mgbake mgbe dashboard rapaara n'otu event loop ahụ). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` na-amanye ka mmekọrịta rụọ ọrụ. Nchekwa/nhichapụ ọnụahịa ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke anaghị eme ihe n'okpuru mmachi nke nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'oge mmepe, na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (na sekọnd) maka ihe mmezi context-window nke na-emezi onwe ya (5004): ọ na-edobe windo ndị provider kwupụtara site na nchọpụta `/models` dịka mgbanwe `auto:discovery` mgbe ha dị iche na katalọgụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. Ọ na-eji data emekọrịtalarị ọzọ (enweghị fetch ọhụrụ); ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-edegharị mgbanwe `manual`.                                                                                                                                                            |

| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(efu)_                                | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa, nke a na-ewere dị ka ndị “local” (aha ọrụ Docker, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relay ndị ọzọ na-abụghị nke mbụ, nke na-anabata naanị
client Claude Code. OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrịọ ka relay ndị ahụ nwee ike ịnabata ha. Ọ bụrụ na naanị ihe ị chọrọ bụ iji
Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ na ị maghị ihe relay ndị a bụ, hapụ nke a ka ọ bụrụ nke agbanyụghị ma tinye provider
Anthropic-compatible nkịtị kama.

## 21. Ahụike Proxy

| Variable                                          | Default                  | Source File                                                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                           | Oge ngwụcha maka nyocha ahụike fast-fail.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                            | Oghere oge (awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxy ndị a na-atụle n'ime atụmatụ pool ahaziri maka latency.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                           | TTL cache nke nsonaazụ nyocha ahụike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                           | TTL cache maka nyocha ahụike proxy ndị dara. Debe nke a ka ọ dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka e wee nwalee ọzọ ngwa ngwa mgbe oge proxy gwụsịrị nwa oge n'okpuru concurrency dị elu, n'emeghị ka fast-fail kwụsị ịrụ ọrụ maka proxy ndị nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji gbanyụọ background proxy health scheduler nke na-enyocha proxy ndị edebanyere aha mgbe ụfọdụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Oge n'etiti nyocha zuru ezu nke background health-scheduler na ms (opekempe `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Oge etiti ngafe-mgbake n’azụ na ms: ugboro ole a na-enyocha proxy ndị na-adịghị mma na mbụ ọzọ, ka proxy gbakere wee laghachi na ntụgharị n’enweghị mmalitegharị. Uru ndị dị n’okpuru `60000` ga-alaghachi na uru ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                               | Ebumnuche nyocha nnweta nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-eji. Tụnye ya aka na URL nke ime/netwọkụ nke gị iji zere nke ọha bụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                               | Ọnụọgụ nyocha a na-amalite n’otu oge n’ime batch ọ bụla, nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. A na-edobe opekempe ya na 1 ma na-amachi ya na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                               | Oge nchere na ms n’etiti ọpụpụ nyocha abụọ n’ime otu batch. Na-enweghị ya, batch ahụ dum ga-apụ n’otu oge, IP ọpụpụ ha na-ekekọrịta nwekwara ike ime ka ebumnuche nwere mmachi ọnụego kwụsị ha. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ oghere oge ahụ; a na-amachi ya na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ "false" iji kwụsị inyocha ezigbo host nke provider e kenyere proxy (`GET /models`, enweghị API key), ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), nyocha nnweta akpaaka (scheduler + bọtịnụ "Test All" nke `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) bụ **naanị-ọgụgụ**, ọ dịghịkwa mgbe ha na-edegharị status proxy — naanị operator na-ahọrọ active/inactive, ka nyocha na-adịghị akwụsi ike ghara ime ka proxy e kenyere gharazi ịrụ ọrụ (#6246). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachite omume ochie nke nnwale-na-ntọala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                 | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya dị na DB nke dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-emegharị mbọ maka ntụgharị streaming ndị tọgbọ chakoo site n’ụzọ credential nkịtị (ruo na mbọ ọzọ `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX`) kama igosi 200 tọgbọ chakoo ma ọ bụ 502 nwere ọdịnaya tọgbọ chakoo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                 | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya dị na DB nke dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nleba anya naanị-ọgụgụ nke ọpụpụ pool n’okpuru proxy pool dị na dashboard (IP ọpụpụ dị iche iche, njikọ, na nke a hụrụkarị n’azụ otu IP n’ime awa 24 gara aga, site na proxy log). A naghị eji ya eme routing ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                 | Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ a na-ahọrọ iji ya (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); mgbanwe DB nke dashboard nwere ikike karịa). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anabata adreesị ndị a hụrụ nwere ụbọchị nke onye ọrụ zipụrụ maka onye otu pool ọ bụla ma jikọta ha na ndekọ journal a gụrụ maka ngosipụta na nhazi pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                                | Oge nlele n'ime awa maka ahịrị egress ndị onye ọrụ zipụrụ (a na-agụ ya mgbe ọ bụla a na-agụ; ọ dịghị mkpa ịmalitegharịa). A machibidoro ya n'etiti 1..168; uru dị n'èzí oke ahụ, nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ nke tọgbọ chakoo ga-alaghachi na 24 site n'otu ịdọ aka ná ntị maka usoro ahụ dum, ọ gaghị atụpụ njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka scheduler wepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka mgbe ọdịda dị iche iche mere n'usoro ugboro ugboro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda na-esochi ibe ha tupu scheduler ewepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka (mgbe `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka scheduler gbanyụọ proxy n'ụzọ dị nro (ọnọdụ `dead`, anaghị ehichapụ ya ma ọlị) mgbe ọdịda dị iche iche mere n'usoro ugboro ugboro, kama iwepụ ya. Nke a bụ nhọrọ na-adịghị emebi ihe kama `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proxy ahụ na-apụ ozugbo na mkpebi pool/rotation (nyo ọnọdụ alive nke mkpebi scope-pool ji eme ihe ewepụlarị ya), a ga-emegharịkwa ya na-akpaghị aka ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe nyocha ọzọ. Ọ na-eji otu oke `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ọ bụrụ na ọkọlọtọ abụọ ahụ bụ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ga-emeri.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                 | Nhọrọ ịwụfe nyocha n'ụzọ passive: mgbe ọ bụ `true`, scheduler na-awụfe nyocha live maka endpoint nke okporo ụzọ production ya na nso nso a gosipụtarala na ọ dị mma (ọ dịkarịa ala otu ihe ịga nke ọma na enweghị ọdịda proxy e kenyere n'ime window ahụ, ma hụ naanị otu provider). Ọ dị mkpụmkpụ karịa oge sweep nke nkeji 10 ka ọdịda mere na nso nso a wee nwee mmetụta kachasị kama ịbụ nke a gwakọtara ma belata ike ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                                   | Kwe ka usoro OAuth na nkwado provider gafere proxy e kegidere ma jikọọ ozugbo mgbe nyocha izizi nke nnweta proxy dara. Usoro ikike dị irè bụ mgbanwe Feature Flags DB > env var > uru ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

A na-akwụsị ngụ oge ahụ ozugbo ọrụ malitere ịrụ — `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS` na-amachi oge mmezu n'ụzọ dị iche. Env na-edobe naanị ndabara: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` echekwara, emesịa `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` nke njikọ ọ bụla; nke ọ bụla n'ime ha na-akarị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (nkeji 10)      | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                            | Oke ogologo oge arịrịọ anabatara nwere ike ịnọ na mmezu tupu ndoputa mmachi ọsọ ya kubie ume — e kewapụrụ ya na atụmatụ oge ichere n'ahịrị ka mbido fetch dị nwayọ na gateway ndị na-abụghị incremental ghara ịgafe oge (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (agbanyụrụ)          | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                            | Oke nnabata n'ahịrị: jiri 429 `queue_full` jụ arịrịọ ma ọ bụrụ na ọnụọgụ arịrịọ ndị a adịlarị n'ahịrị eruola nke a. `0` = enweghị oke (ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(edobeghị)_             | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                            | Manye ụgbụ nchekwa mmachi ọsọ nke na-agbanye onwe ya ka ọ dị ọkụ/gbanyụọ n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard echekwara. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji manye ya ka ọ dị ọkụ, na `false`/`0`/`off` iji manye ya ka ọ gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara (ọ na-ekpuchi ụfọdụ ọrụ Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker). | `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                     | Oge izu ike kacha nta (ms) tupu anwa onye na-eweta ọrụ/njikọ dara ada ọzọ. Ọ na-abawanye n'ụdị exponential dịka ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha si dị.
