| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (nkeji 5)       | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                     | Oke oge izu ike kachasị (ms) tupu anwa onye na-eweta ọrụ/njikọ dara ada ọzọ n'agbanyeghị ọnọdụ. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(edobeghị → gbanyụrụ)_  | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mmalite) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro arụmọrụ) | **Gịnị:** iweghachi stream upstream ndị e bipụrụ n'ụzọ doro anya nye onye ọrụ (port free-claude-code). Ọ na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ka e wee meghee ma nwaa ọzọ n'ụzọ onye ọrụ na-agaghị ahụ ma ọ bụrụ na nkwụsị _tupu nkwenye_ — nke mere tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** upstream ndị na-adịghị akwụsi ike/ndị na-ebipụkarị stream na byte 0 mgbe stream na-amalite; hapụ ya ka ọ GBANYỤỌ ma ọ bụrụ na ị gaghị anagide mgbakwunye ruru 750 ms n'ogologo oge tupu token mbụ apụta na stream ọ bụla. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Ọ na-etinye ntọala Resilience echekwara nke mbụ; ntọala Dashboard na-akarị ya ozugbo edobere ya. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(edoghị ya → gbanyụọ)_  | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro arụmọrụ)  | **Gịnị:** ịga n'ihu n'etiti stream (Fase 4.4) — mgbe mbelata _post-commit_ gasịrị (bytes eruolarị onye ahịa), jiri ederede nke ezughị ezu dịka assistant prefill rịọ ọzọ ma jikọta suffix na-efu efu. Ọ bụ naanị maka streams OpenAI-compatible nke plain-text; ọ naghị arụ ọrụ mgbe tool call ka na-aga n'ihu. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** maka mmepụta ogologo a na-akwụsị n'etiti azịza ma ị nabata ka ọdụ ederede eweghachiri bịarute dịka otu burst kama iji token n'otu n'otu. Ọ nọọrọ onwe ya na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe ize ndụ dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(edoghị ya → gbanyụọ)_  | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                       | Watchdog nke useful-output maka active-stream a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye. Ọ na-achọpụta streams ndị na-aga n'ihu izipu chunks mana nọgide n'okpuru ọsọ assistant-output ahaziri; heartbeats, usage events, empty deltas, na usoro tool/reasoning anaghị eme ka a ghọta ha dịka ọganihu. Ọ dị iche na idle timeouts na hard-deadline timeouts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                         | Window useful-output na-akpụgharị, nke a kpaara ókè na 1000–600000 ms; a chọrọ otu window zuru ezu tupu ịkwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                         | Ọsọ byte kacha nta nke assistant-output UTF-8 (ihe nnọchi anya token e ji akpachapụ anya), nke a kpaara ókè na 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                         | Sample useful-output kacha nta na-abụghị efu a na-ewere na enwere ike ịtụ ya, nke a kpaara ókè na bytes 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                      | Nkwụsịtụ dị n'etiti healthchecks nke token ndị provider mgbe a na-amalite (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                      | Jitter kacha nta ahọpụtara na-enweghị usoro (ms) a na-agbakwunye n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti healthchecks nke token ndị provider, iji gbochie izipu ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                      | Jitter kacha ukwuu ahọpụtara na-enweghị usoro (ms) a na-agbakwunye n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti healthchecks nke token ndị provider, iji gbochie izipu ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                      | Ogo batch maka concurrent-check n'oge nyocha token-healthcheck nke mmalite; ụkpụrụ ndị ka ukwuu na-enyocha njikọ ndị ọzọ n'otu oge, ebe ụkpụrụ ndị ka nta na-ebelata burst load (Issue #7875, regression nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                           | Ọnụọgụ retries akpaghị aka na nzaghachi cooldown metụtara model tupu eweghachitere onye ahịa njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                           | Oke kachasị nke etiti oge ndọghachi-azụ (sekọnd) n’etiti nnwale ọzọ mgbe oge nchere gasịrị. Uru a na-amachi ya n’agbanyeghị `Retry-After` sitere na upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                       | URL proxy Headroom na-echekwa token. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-ebido CLI `headroom-ai` mpaghara na loopback na ndabara; gbanwee ya naanị iji tụnye aka na proxy Docker sidecar mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### Konstantị nhazi mgbake stream

A na-eji konstantị ndị edere kpọmkwem n'ime koodu dị na
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`) hazie omume njigide mgbake ahụ, dịka e gosiri ebe a maka ntụaka —
ịgbanwe ha chọrọ idezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — ogologo oge a na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe
  ka e nwee ike ịnwale ọzọ ma ọ bụrụ na e bepụ ya n’oge tupu e ziga byte ọ bụla nye client.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — oke siri ike maka windo ahụ e jidere; mee commit
  (flush + passthrough) ozugbo byte ruru ọnụọgụ a, n’agbanyeghị timer.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — oke kachasị nke mmegharị mmeghe upstream
  stream n'ụzọ client na-agaghị ahụ, mgbe emebeghị commit nke njigide ahụ.

> **Oke ọsọ sliding-window nke provider ọ bụla (enweghị env var):** _fallback_ oke ọsọ
> sliding-window nke provider ọ bụla, nke e si FCC bugharịa, dị n'ime koodu
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, nke ejikọtara site na
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana a na-ebupụta ya na **map ndabara efu**
> ma ọ **nweghị env var maka operator** ugbu a — a na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ naanị site na test hook /
> ndezi koodu. E bu n’obi ka a ghara idepụta ya na tebụl dị n’elu. Limiter relay nke `(token, IP)`
> ọ bụla nke _nwere_ knob bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Netwọk & Ports).

---

## 22. Nchọpụta na ndozi nsogbu

> [!CAUTION]
> Ngbanwe ndị a na-emepụta **mmepụta zuru ezu** ma nwee ike ikpughe data dị nzuzo. **Agbanyela ha mgbe ọ bụla na gburugburu mmepụta.**

| Ngbanwe                               | Ndabara                                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tọọ `1` iji gbanye ndekọ zuru ezu nke Cursor executor (iberibe SSE a tụgharịrị, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Aha ọzọ nke `CURSOR_DEBUG` nke dakọtara na nsụgharị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-anata iberibe Cursor raw a tụgharịrị mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge njedebe nke stream mgbe ọ naghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge mgbaghara (ms) mgbe composer kv_after_text soft terminator gasịrị ma ọ bụrụ na byte ka dị na buffer — nke a na-enye oku ngwaọrụ exec_mcp nke na-esote oge iji mezue frame ya.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | agbanyere (`!== "0"`)                      | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ntuziaka tool-commit nke na-eme ka composer-2.5 kpọọ ngwaọrụ n'ụzọ a pụrụ ịdabere na ya. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Ederede system prompt nke onye ọrụ sistemụ kọwara, nke a na-agbakwunye na system message MGBE ntụgharị gasịrị (ntinye mgbe ntụgharị gasịrị), ma rute n'ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka mbido ntuziaka a na-ewepụ na ngọngọ system preamble ndị e weghachiri. Hapụ ya ka edoghị ya iji gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                            | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Tọọ `1` iji wepụ ngọngọ system-prompt preamble ndị e weghachiri site na mmalite nke stream openai→claude. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — usoro nyocha ndị ahụ dabere n'ụdị ederede Bekee ma na-agbanwe payload nzaghachi, ya mere nzaghachi nke malitere n'ụzọ ziri ezi site na ngalaba dị otu ahụ ga-efunahụ ya.                  |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                                    | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Oge njedebe nbudata maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ntinye ọhụhụ `image_url` dị n'ime ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(a na-enyocha)_                           | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Dochie ọchụchọ Cursor IDE state DB a na-eji achọpụta nsụgharị IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(chọpụta / kpọgide)_                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | NJ build nke Agent CLI (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) maka `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` na Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_BIN`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ụzọ gaa na Cursor Agent binary a na-eji emepụta onyonyo. Mgbe edoghị ya, handler na-eji `providerSpecificData.agentBin`, mesịa PATH.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CURSOR_IMG_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `210000`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor Agent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MAX_CONCURRENT`           | `2`                                        | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ụzọ njikwa ọrụ na-aga n'otu oge n'oche a na-ekekọrịta maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MODEL`                    | arịrịọ / `auto`                            | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Dochie `--model` nke Cursor CLI maka ọrụ onyonyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Ugboro nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `60000`                                    | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka njedebe nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `3000`                                     | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Ugboro nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka njedebe nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CURSOR_DATA_DIR`                     | _(a na-enyocha)_                           | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | Dochie ndekọ data Cursor Agent CLI (`…/versions/<id>`); ọ bụ otu ngbanwe ahụ agent gọọmentị na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CURSOR_TOKEN`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `scripts/ad-hoc/cursor-tap.cjs`                                                                       | Token bearer Cursor kpọmkwem nke ngwa ọrụ ndị nrụpụta na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_REQUEST_SHAPE`         | agbanyụrụ (a ga-ahọrọ iji `"1"` gbanye ya) | `src/app/api/v1/chat/completions/route.ts`                                                            | Dekọọ akara content-type/length maka payload nkata buru ibu mgbe atọrọ `"1"`. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji belata mkpọtụ ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEBUG_RESPONSES_SSE_TO_JSON`         | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji dekọọ nkọwa ntụgharị Responses API SSE→JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DEBUG_CLAUDE_NONSTREAM`              | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji gosipụta iberibe textContent efu n'ụzọ ntụgharị nzaghachi Claude (naanị maka nchọpụta nsogbu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_E2E_MODE`      | _(edoghị ya)_                              | Ngwa nnwale E2E                                                                                       | Tọọ `true` iji gbanye ọnọdụ nnwale E2E (nkwenye e mere ka ọ dị mfe, njikọ nnwale).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## 23. Njikọta GitHub

Kwe ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkọ nsogbu ozugbo site na Dashboard.

| Ihe Mgbanwe           | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | GitHub Personal Access Token nwere ikike `issues:write`.                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(edobeghị)_ | nhazi mbido nke nsogbu / ihe enyemaka cloud agent | Token nnweta GitHub izugbe a na-eji dị ka ndabere maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, nke ihe enyemaka cloud agent dị na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` na-ejikwa. |

---

## Ọnọdụ Mbuga

Maka ntuziaka SRE nke backend relay (omume ts/bifrost/auto, ebe a ga-edowe 9router ma e jiri ya tụnyere CLIProxyAPI, na atụmatụ ndabere maka oke ọsọ mbufe), lee [Atụmatụ Backend Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Obodo Dịkarịsịrị Mfe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Mmepụta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Enweghị Njikọ Ịntanetị / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS nwere Proxy Ntụgharị (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Ebe Nnwale Nchekwa Skills (v3.8.0+)

Oke na ntọala nchekwa ndị a na-etinye mgbe framework Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme automation ndị ọrụ kọwara n'ime gburugburu e kewapụrụ maka nnwale.

| Ihe Mgbanwe                       | Ndabara                                               | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke oge wall-clock maka oge mmejuputa nke ọ bụla nke koodu skill dị na sandbox. Nke a bụ oke siri ike; a ga-akwụsị ihe ọ bụla gafere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(na-eji `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`` dị ka ndabere)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oke oge maka nhazi mmejuputa skill n'ogo dị elu. Tọọ ya karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ nwere ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị skill nwere ike ịgụ site na otu faịlụ ọ bụla dị na sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                                     | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị a ga-edekọ site na otu nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke siri ike nke mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr a na-eweghachi site na otu ọkpụkpọ sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka njikọ netwọk na-apụ apụ site n'ime sandbox. Ndabara ya bụ **nkewapụrụ** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta onyonyo container ndị e kwere maka mmejuputa sandbox, nke rikoma kewara. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ pụtara na ọ bụ naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ka a ga-eji.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime)_                            | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Onyonyo container a na-eji mgbe a na-emepụta sandbox Docker na-akwado. Gbanwee ya iji kpọchie onyonyo ntọala omenala e wusiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                                | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime container maka idobe skill na sandbox: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ runtime arụnyere kacha mma dabere na OS nke host (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), ma na-eji Docker dị ka ndabere ma ọ bụrụ na ndị ahụ adịghị. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ime ka `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` rụọ ọrụ na-emepe ụzọ egress site na koodu skill ọ bụla. Jikọtara ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana amụma `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike n'ebe mbugote ọtụtụ ndị na-eji ọnụ.

## 25. Oke Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ, Ọwara Netwọk, Nkwado Ndabere & Ihe Runtime Ndị Ọzọ

Ebe njedebe oke ndị na-enye ọrụ, ọwara netwọk (Tailscale, Ngrok, proxy nyocha MITM), ọdọ egress 1Proxy, nkwado ndabere nke nchekwa data na obere mgbanwe pụrụ iche maka atụmatụ ọ bụla nke oyi akwa executor ma ọ bụ scripts na-eji.

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi                                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Eriri njikọ Redis maka backend nke rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host mmepụta)_                                                            | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Dochie host a na-eji enweta oke coding-plan Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | ewepụtara site na host                                                      | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Mgbanwe URL oke zuru ezu maka Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie nnọkọ console maka ụzọ mbata oke Token Plan nkeonwe nke Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (igodo inference API enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo header arịrịọ `Cookie` dum — ọ nwere `login_qwencloud_ticket` — site na oku `api.json` ọ bụla gaa na `cs-data.qwencloud.com` na home.qwencloud.com › Ịgba Ụgwọ › Ndebanye Aha (F12 › Netwọk). Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo ma metụta naanị nnọkọ ahụ; họrọ iji oghere Dashboard `qwenCloudCookie` nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                   |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe aka nke `sec_token` maka ụzọ mbata console Token Plan. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe edoghị ya, fetcher na-enweta ya site na HTML dashboard site n'iji cookie ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe host ụzọ mbata maka fetcher oke Token Plan nkeonwe (dịka `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` maka console Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL dashboard a na-eji enweta `sec_token` site na HTML nke abanyerela.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Mgbanwe ụzọ front-end console maka inweta oke vision/media nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Mgbanwe ụzọ front-end nke console maka iweta oke ojiji efu multimodal nke Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_AUDIO_FE_PATH`              | `/costing-balance/free-quota-audio`                                         | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Mgbanwe ụzọ front-end nke console maka iweta oke ojiji efu nke ọdịyo Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_ALLOWLIST_PATH`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierAllowlist.ts`                           | Ụzọ nhọrọ gaa na faịlụ JSON mpaghara nke ga-anọchi ndepụta nnabata e wuru n'ime ya maka ụdịdị ederede nke ọkwa efu Alibaba. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọ ga-eji `$DATA_DIR/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`, ma emesịa `config/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CONTEXT_RESERVE_TOKENS`                       | `1024`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Token ndị edobere maka mmepụta mmecha mgbe a na-agbakọ oke prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ onyonyo inline kachasị ọhụrụ a ga-edobe mgbe a na-ewepụ ndị ochie ka ha daba na windo context (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MODEL_ALIAS_COMPAT_ENABLED`                   | agbanyere                                                                   | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                                              | Gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ oyi akwa ndakọrịta model-alias ochie nke ndị ahịa ochie na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | agbanyere                                                                   | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Tọọ `false` (ma ọ bụ `0`) iji gbanyụọ fallback mberede mgbe budget gwụrụ, nke na-ebugharị arịrịọ ndị dara ada gaa na ụdịdị efu `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b`. Usoro mkpa dị irè bụ mgbanwe Feature Flags DB > env var > ndabara; ọ bụrụ na nke ahụ adịghị, ọrụ ahụ ga-eji uru env ahụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMMAND_CODE_CALLBACK_PORT`                   | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | `src/app/api/providers/command-code/auth/shared.ts`                       | Port mpaghara a na-eji maka callback ndị yiri OAuth sitere na enyemaka Command Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Uru a na-eziga dị ka header `x-command-code-version` gaa na upstream Command Code. Gbanwee ya iji bulie ụdị CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `COMMANDCODE_API_URL`                          | `https://api.commandcode.ai`                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/command-code.ts`                                 | URL ntọala maka upstream ojiji/oke Command Code nke telemetry nke onye na-eweta oke smartphone na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka Command Code API nke a na-akwado n'onwe ma ọ bụ nke ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MITM_LOCAL_PORT`                              | `443`                                                                       | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri njikọ mpaghara maka proxy ndozi mperi MITM.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MITM_DISABLE_TLS_VERIFY`                      | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ nkwenye TLS nke upstream (maka mmepe naanị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MITM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | `60000`                                                                     | `src/mitm/socketTimeouts.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                       | Oge ngwụcha socket na-adịghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka njikọ ndị a na-ebufe site na proxy; a na-akwatu socket ndị gafere oge a ka a ghara ịhapụ tunnel ndị meghere ọkara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `BRIDGE_PORT`                                  | `20129`                                                                     | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebe Antigravity MITM bridge na-ege ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ROUTER_URL`                                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1/antigravity`                                     | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Ebe njedebe router nke bridge na-ebuga okporo ụzọ Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CERT_DIR`                                     | `~/.omniroute/mitm`                                                         | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Ndekọ nwere `server.key`/`server.crt` maka onye na-ege TLS nke bridge; bridge ahụ ga-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na nke ọ bụla adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MITM_VERBOSE`                                 | `1`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`, `src/mitm/_internal/bypass.cjs`                    | Ọkwa nkọwa ndekọ mkpebi nhazi ụzọ: `0` na-eme ka ọ daa jụụ, ụkpụrụ ndị dị elu na-edekọ mkpebi bypass/ụzọ karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED`                         | `false`                                                                     | `src/mitm/manager.ts`                                                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji họrọ iji ụdị asambodo root-CA + per-host-leaf (#6684). Nrụnye ọhụrụ na-enweta ya na-akpaghị aka; nrụnye nwere asambodo legacy leaf a tụkwasịrị obi nke dịbu ga-edobe asambodo fixed-SAN ochie ma ọ bụrụ na ahọrọghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MITM_CERT_MODE`                               | `legacy`                                                                    | `src/mitm/manager.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                              | Onye njikwa MITM na-esetịpụ nke a MAKA usoro proxy ọ malitere (`root-ca` \| `legacy`) — ọ na-egosipụta mkpebi mbugharị asambodo; e kwesịghị iji aka setịpụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_KEY`                        | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Alias maka `BIFROST_API_KEY` (scripts ndị na-agụ env site na `OMNIROUTE_*` na-eji ya). `BIFROST_API_KEY` na-ebute ụzọ mgbe edobere ha abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND`                      | `ts` / `auto`                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Backend relay maka `/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`: `ts \| bifrost \| auto`. `ts` = relay TypeScript (ndabara mgbe ahazighị Bifrost); `auto` na-ahọrọ Bifrost mgbe edobere `BIFROST_BASE_URL` ma `BIFROST_ENABLED` ≠ `0`, tinyere fallback TS akpaka ma ọ bụrụ na enweghị ike iru sidecar; `bifrost` na-amanye Bifrost (siri ike, enweghị fallback). Auth/rate-limit/injection-guard/allowlist na-agba ọsọ mgbe niile na route Next tupu ihe ndị ọzọ. Response na-ebu `X-Routing-Backend` / `X-Routing-Fallback` / `X-Routing-Fallback-Reason`. |
| `RELAY_ROUTING_BACKEND`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Aha ọzọ a nabatara maka `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` (otu ụkpụrụ `ts \| bifrost \| auto` ahụ). `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` na-ebute ụzọ mgbe edobere ha abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS`        | `5000`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrostCooldown.ts`                | Oge nchere (ms) mgbe ngafe Bifrost sidecar dara na ọnọdụ `auto` tupu relay ahụ anwa sidecar ahụ ọzọ; ọ na-eziga ozugbo n'ụzọ TS ka oge nchere ahụ na-adị, wee nwalee ọzọ. `0` na-agbanyụ ya. Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ faịlụ asambodo PEM TLS iji nye `omniroute serve` ọrụ site na HTTPS (ya na `--tls-cert` bụ otu ihe). A ga-ejikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; sava kwụụrụ onwe ya ga-akwụsị TLS n'otu listener ahụ (`wss://` ga-arụ ọrụ na-enweghị mgbanwe). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya → HTTP nkịtị. Inye naanị otu n'ime cert/key, ma ọ bụ ụzọ faịlụ a na-apụghị ịgụ, ga-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị ma nọgide na HTTP.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ faịlụ igodo nzuzo PEM TLS maka HTTPS nke `omniroute serve` (ya na `--tls-key` bụ otu ihe). A ga-ejikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Lee `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED`            | `0`                                                                         | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ụzọ `/api/local/*`. Mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ na ọ bụ `0`, ụzọ `/api/local/*` niile na-eweghachi 503 na production. Ọ ga-abụrịrị `1` na deploy ndị na-abụghị loopback iji mee ka Redis launcher na ndị mbido ọrụ mpaghara yiri ya nke otu ọpịpị rụọ ọrụ. Nchedo okpukpu abụọ ya na nhazi route-guard nke `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` dị na `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Bearer token maka ndị na-akpọ `/api/local/*` na-esighị na loopback (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ngwa desktop). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ sitere na IP ndị na-abụghị loopback ga-eburịrị `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. A chọrọ ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` na deployment ndị na-abụghị loopback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_CONTAINER_NAME`               | `omniroute-redis`                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Aha container maka Redis launcher nke otu ọpịpị (`omniroute redis up`). | `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_HOST_PORT`                    | `6379`                                                                      | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Host port maka Redis launcher nke otu ọpịpị. Port ime container ahụ ka ga-abụ 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Host interface nke Redis launcher nke otu ọpịpị na-ebipụta na ya. | `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Host interface ebe docker-compose na-ebipụta Redis sidecar (#9286). Redis nke compose na-arụ ọrụ na-enweghị `requirepass`; container ngwa na-enweta ya site na netwọkụ compose (`redis:6379`) — port e bipụtara dị naanị maka tooling dị n'akụkụ host. `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere LAN niile Redis na-enweghị nyocha njirimara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Port host maka Redis sidecar nke compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Onye Nhazi Oge Mmalite Claude

Mmalite nke cron na-achịkwa maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ndị kwadoro ya, ka e wee jiri obere arịrịọ ahaziri n'oge mepee windo oke ọnụego awa 5 kama iji ezigbo arịrịọ mbụ mepee ya (#8848). Onye nhazi oge a na-anọ n'ọnọdụ agbanyụrụ ma ọ bụrụ na `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` abụghị uru eziokwu **ma** etinyeghị ọkọlọtọ na njikọ ahụ n'ime `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ efu pụtara na ọ dịghị ihe a ga-amalite ọbụlagodi mgbe agbanyere env var ahụ.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(edoghị → agbanyụrụ)_      | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Isi mgba ọkụ maka onye nhazi oge mmalite. Ọ na-anabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ọ naghị ele nnukwu ma ọ bụ obere mkpụrụedemede anya, a na-ewepụkwa oghere n'akụkụ). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, na-ahapụ onye nhazi oge ahụ ka ọ bụrụ agbanyụrụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nkwupụta cron nwere mpaghara ise maka oge mmalite ọ bụla, nke a na-enyocha na `America/Los_Angeles` (mpaghara oge nrụgharị nke Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere nke sava nnabata.                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ọnụọgụ njikọ a na-amalite n'otu oge n'oge mmalite ọ bụla. A na-amachi ya n'etiti `1`-`10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-alaghachi na `3`.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model a na-eji maka arịrịọ mmalite. Gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ndabara adịghị na atụmatụ gị; họrọ model kacha ọnụ ala nke ka nwere ike imepee windo ahụ.                                                                                                        |

### Oge VNC Maka Nbanye Ihe Nchọgharị & Aha Ọzọ Maka Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC dị n'ime akpa a na-eji maka ijide ozi nzere site na nbanye ihe nchọgharị nke onye ọrụ na-emekọrịta ihe (`/api/vnc-session`), tinyere aha ọzọ ochie `DATA_DIR`. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara VNC na-ezo aka na image `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` agụnyere, a na-agbanwekwa ha naanị maka image akpa ahaziri iche, port, ma ọ bụ nhazi usoro ndụ ya.

| Ngbanwe                               | Ndabara                                                                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tag oyiyi Docker maka konteena nbanye Chromium+VNC. Wuo `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ mee ka nke a rụtụ aka n'oyiyi ahaziri iche.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Faịlụ binary nke sistemụ konteena eji ebido konteena VNC (dịka ọmụmaatụ, tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `podman`).                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọdụ VNC/noVNC ekpughere n'ime konteena ahụ.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọdụ Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime konteena ahụ.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ ndekọ profaịlụ Chromium dị n'ime konteena ahụ.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ dị na kọmputa nnabata nke na-edobe profaịlụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (nkeji 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngafe n'ihi enweghị ọrụ (ms) tupu ewepụ nnọkọ VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oke siri ike (ms) maka ogologo oge otu nnọkọ VNC nwere ike ịdị.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke nnọkọ VNC nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngafe (ms) maka ichere ka ihe nchọgharị dị na konteena dị njikere maka CDP.                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngafe (ms) maka ịnakọta nnọkọ/cookies e jidere mgbe nbanye mechara.                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Flag ahịrị iwu ndị ọzọ a na-enyefe Chromium dị na konteena.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche nke konteena nbanye VNC na-esonye na ya (#12571) kama bridge ndabara, ka konteena ndị ibe ya ghara iru ọdụ bridge CDP ya.                                                   |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edoghị ya)_                                                            | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Aha nnọchi ochie** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edoghị ma `DATA_DIR` ma `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`. Ọ na-achọta cache nnọkọ web-thread Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |
