## 26. Nnwale & Ngwaọrụ E2E

Ndị a bụ ndị `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs` na-eji. Ahapụla
uru niile dị n'okpuru n'enweghị nhazi na mwepụta prodọkshọn.

| Mgbanwe                                | Ndabara                                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọnọdụ mmalite E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | na-eji `INITIAL_PASSWORD` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Okwuntughe onye nchịkwa a na-etinye n'ime gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ nyocha ugboro ugboro nke healthcheck mpaghara mgbe Playwright na-agba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ usoro nyocha ugboro ugboro nke healthcheck token OAuth n'oge ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(edoghị ya)_                                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ndị na-eweta ọrụ, nke e ji rikoma kewaa, a ga-ewepụ na nyocha mweghachi token a na-eme tupu oge eruo (dịka `codex,openai`). Ọ bụ ụzọ ọzọ ezubere iche kama ịgbanyụ healthcheck kpamkpam — ndị na-eweta ọrụ nwere TTL dị mkpụmkpụ ga-anọgide na-emelite, ebe ndị na-eweta cascade ga-emeghachi omume naanị mgbe ọ dị mkpa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ healthcheck na stdout nke Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mafere mwube prodọkshọn Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (njikarịcha CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                              | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Mafere hook iwepụ OmniRoute (CI na-eji ya edobe `node_modules` ka ọ ghara ịgbanwe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                                         | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Oge nchere (ms) ka sava nwee ezi ọnọdụ tupu ịgba ule ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL nke ngwaọrụ smoke Electron na-atụ anya ka ngwa a kwakọtara nye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ngụkọta oge ngwụcha (ms) tupu ngwaọrụ smoke kwụsị ịnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Oge nkwụsị (ms) mgbe ibe ahụ kwụsịrị ibudata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(akpaka)_                                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ akọwapụtara nke faịlụ executable Electron a kwakọtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ndekọ data maka ịgba ule smoke Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji chekwaa ndekọ data smoke mgbe arụmọrụ ahụ gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ ndekọ Electron ozugbo na stdout n'oge arụmọrụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: malitegharịa site n'iji otu ndekọ data ahụ ma gosi na mmalite nke abụọ họọrọ draịva SQLite nke sistemụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(nchọgharị PATH)_                               | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Dochie ụzọ faịlụ binari Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Usoro ntụgharị asụsụ dọkụmentị

Nke `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` na-eji (iwu `npm run i18n:run`).
Agbanwebeghị mgbanwe ise niile na ndabara — tọọ ha na `.env` naanị na kọmputa
ndị kwesịrị inwe ike ịrụ onye ntụgharị dọkụmentị ahụ.

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL ntọala kwekọrọ na OpenAI maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ.                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Token Bearer maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ (anaghị edekọ ya ma ọlị).                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | NJ model, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`      | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Oge ngwụcha arịrịọ ọ bụla na milisekọnd.                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Arịrịọ ntụgharị asụsụ ndị na-aga n'otu oge mgbe a na-arụ ọrụ n'elu ọtụtụ faịlụ / mpaghara asụsụ. |

## 27. Nri Radar (Nnabata n'Onwe Gị)

Mgbakwunye nhọrọ nke ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED na-achịkwa (agbanyụrụ na ndabara — ọkọlọtọ njirimara
a na-agbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Ngbanwe anọ mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ iji dochie ntọala maka nri a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara,
yana usoro igodo ndị nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị uru ndabara nke na-eduga na
ogwe ọrụ nzuzo nke onye nwe ya. Lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka akwụkwọ zuru ezu
banyere modul ahụ na
[usoro ịgbalite ya site na mmalite ruo na njedebe na nhazi a na-eduzi](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

A naghị eji environment variable ahazi ngwa ọgụgụ ọkwa Home/Changelog izugbe,
ọ dịghịkwa adabere na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-eji naanị GET agụ URL `news.json`
nke repository ọha, nke akọwapụtara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts`;
ID nke ihe a jụrụ ka dị na nchekwa mpaghara nke ihe nchọgharị.

| Variable                      | Ndabara                                      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL ntọala nke katalọgụ, ntụnye, onyinye ndị nkwado, na nri Intel ndị a bịanyere aka na ha iche iche na-ekekọrịta. Dochie ya ka ọ na-atụ aka na ọrụ a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara e debere kpọmkwem)_          | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Igodo ọha Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) e ji enyocha mbinye aka nri sitere na nri ahaziri iche.                                                                                            |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye nyere aka" na-emepe (usoro ịnata igodo onye nkwado site na GitHub OAuth).                                                                                     |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Kwado ọrụ ahụ" na-emepe (ibe ịkwụ ụgwọ/atụmatụ).                                                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ nzuzo nke ogwe ọrụ maka naanị onye nwe ya. A chọrọ HTTPS, ma e wezụga maka nnyefe SSH loopback nke HTTP; uru edoghị ma ọ bụ nke na-ezighi ezi anaghị emepụta ihe nchọgharị ọ bụla.           |

---

## Nnyocha: Variable E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghịzi Arụ Ọrụ

Variable ndị a pụtara na ụdị `.env.example` ndị gara aga, mana ha **enweghị ntụaka runtime ọ bụla** na codebase dị ugbu a. E wepụla ha:

| Mgbanwe                                                                                                                                                                         | Ihe kpatara ya                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Faịlụ isi mmalite ọ bụla anaghị agụ ya. SQLite bụ naanị draịva a na-akwado — ọ dịghị mkpa ịhọrọ nke ọ bụla.                                    |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Ọ dị na dọkụmentị ochie/ụkpụrụ env mana anaghị eji ya n'oge arụmọrụ. Ọ nwere ike ịlaghachi na njirimara ọtụtụ-instance n'ọdịnihu.              |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. E nweghị mmachi e ji aka tinye n'ogo nchekwa data.                                                  |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. O yikarịrị ka ewepụla nhicha faịlụ ochie.                                                           |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                        |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding na-eji OAuth, ọ bụghị faịlụ CLI binary).                                   |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | E zoghị aka na ha ebe ọ bụla n'ime koodu isi mmalite.                                                                                          |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ewepụrụ ha na v3.8.0. Runtime anaghịzi agụ env vars ndị a — nzere na-abịa site na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Cursor executor na-eji `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (lee §22).                                                 |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Kiro dị na `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — toggle ya enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.                                         |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na stubs API key ndị ọzọ anaghị eji eme ihe na v3.8.0.                                                                              |

### Ndozi Uru Ndabara

| Mgbanwe                   | Uru `.env.example` Ochie | Ndabara Koodu N'ezie | Ihe Edoziri                                                   |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |

### Mmegharị config OpenCode (ngwaọrụ ad-hoc)

`scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` na-eji ya emegharị `opencode.json`
nwere uru `limit.context` na `limit.output` ziri ezi, nke e wetara site na instance
OmniRoute na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime ndị a achọrọ maka arụmọrụ nkịtị — script a
bụ naanị ngwaọrụ ndị mmepe.

| Mgbanwe            | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | URL ntọala nke instance OmniRoute a ga-ajụ maka `/v1/models`.                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API maka nkwenye njirimara megide endpoint OmniRoute `/v1/models`. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OPENCODE_API_KEY`.            |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API ụdị OpenCode (`sk-...`) nke a na-ede n'ime `opencode.json` e mepụtaghachiri. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OMNIROUTE_KEY`. |

### Ngwa nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị (ngwa ad-hoc)

`scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị, na-eji ya.
Achọghị ya maka ọrụ nkịtị — ọ bụ naanị ngwa ndị nrụpụta.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (efu) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Ozi nzere JSON nke onye njikwa nyere maka provider nke CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị na-anwale (a na-eji `JSON.parse` atụgharị ya). Hapụ ya n'edoghị maka nnwale na-enweghị mmejuputa. |

### Oge Nchọgharị VNC

`src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` na-eji ha ahazi oge Chromium na-enweghị interface eserese nke dabere na Docker maka ndị provider akpaaka nchọgharị. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — e gosiri ndabara n'okpuru.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi                                   | Nkọwa                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ gaa na binary Docker e ji emepụta container VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Image Docker maka container VNC Chromium.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(flags arụnyere n'ime)_       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Arụmụka CLI Chromium ndị ọzọ a na-ebufe na nchọgharị dị n'ime container.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC dị n'ime container.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ profaịlụ dị n'ime container.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ dị n'akụkụ host maka profaịlụ nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe ịnọ nkịtị (ms) tupu a chịkọta oge VNC.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ogologo oge kachasị nke session (ms).                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ session VNC kachasị nwere ike ịrụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge.                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchọgharị ịdị njikere (ms).                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchịkọta/nhicha (ms).                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche container na-esonyere (#12571), kama bridge ndabara. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Ndekọ maka nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke session eriri Notion.               |

### Nkwenye njirimara ọrụ ime

| Mgbanwe                                 | Ndabara | Nkọwa                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –       | Token inline maka nkwenye njirimara site n'otu ọrụ gaa n'ọzọ na management plane.            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –       | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nwere token ọrụ ime (ọ ka mma n'ime container; ọ na-akagbu mgbanwe inline). |

### Ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara   | Nkọwa                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mafere iweta ọnụọgụ OpenRouter nke provider ọ bụla maka ime ka katalọgụ baa ọgaranya. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL cache (ms) maka ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter e wetara.                                                           |

### Njikọ Redis agbakwunyere n'ime

| Mgbanwe                     | Ndabara     | Nkọwa                                                               |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adreesị njikọ maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                          |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Ntọgharị nwere oke OmniRoute maka adreesị njikọ Redis agbakwunyere. |

## 24. Ihe mgbakwunye na mwepụta v3.8.50

E webatara ntọala ndị a mgbe e mechara snapshot nkwekọrịta gburugburu ebe obibi gara aga.

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oge kachasị a ga-echere oghere nnabata nkata dị arọ tupu `503` nke enwere ike ịnwale ọzọ; obere oge nchere nwere oke na-ahazi nrịgo mberede nke ndị agent n'usoro kama inye `503` ozugbo. `0` na-eweghachite ọjụjụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oke bytes e tinyere n'ahịrị maka nchere nnabata: ọ na-amachi mkpokọta bytes nke body echekwara n'ime buffer ma kwụsị n'ofe process niile ka nchere ahụ ghara ịbawanye heap (#4380). Nchere ndị gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` nke enwere ike ịnwale ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | No-op nke kwụsịrị ịbụ nke akwadoro kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ ụzọ nnabata nke session ọ bụla ma jiri otu oke maka process niile dochie ha. A na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | No-op nke kwụsịrị ịbụ nke akwadoro kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ ụzọ nnabata nke session ọ bụla ma jiri otu oke maka process niile dochie ha. A na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (agbanyụrụ)            | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Ụzọ nnabata virtual nke runtime na-agbanwe onwe ha (#9654): mgba ọkụ ukwu maka adaptive gate nke tenant ọ bụla (sistemụ 2). Ọ dị iche na mgbanwe lane nke connection ọ bụla kwụsịrị ịbụ nke akwadoro dị n'elu (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, no-op kemgbe #10110). Feature flag dashboard nwere otu aha ahụ; env var na-aka dashboard override ike; ọ chọrọ restart. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Na-amachi ogologo oge oku run-now ga-echere job na-aga n'ihu tupu ọ malite run e tinyere n'ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na browser-risk dị ọhụrụ site na session Chrome CDP nke account ọ bụla; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-echekwa session Adobe ndị arụziri n'okpuru `DATA_DIR`; tọọ `0` maka ọnọdụ dị naanị na memory.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oghere oge kacha nta n'etiti ntinye generate Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oge nkwụsị ọzọ mgbe ntinye Adobe ọ bụla nke atọ gara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(edoghị ya)_              | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Ihe nzuzo a na-ekekọrịta nke e debanyere site na `setWebhook` ma nyochaa megide header `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` na nnyefe webhook ọ bụla. A chọrọ ya maka ụzọ webhook; ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, a ga-eji 503 jụ nnyefe webhook.                                                                                                                          |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model a na-eji maka nzaghachi nkata Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Mgbanwe URL ntọala Bot API maka proxy ma ọ bụ sava Bot API nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Oge nkwụsị na millisecond maka oku Bot API na-apụ apụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (agbanyere)            | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Tọọ `0` iji ghara imepụta tarball `.tar.gz` mgbe a na-akwadebe pack ML/browser nhọrọ maka osisi Electron standalone (a ka ga-emepụta ndekọ pack na `optional-packs.index.json`). Workflow mwepụta desktop na-eji ya belata nha artifact a na-ebugo.                                                                                                           |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Ntọala ndabara zuru ụwa ọnụ maka browser headless na ọwara tool na-apụ apụ. Uru connection edobere na dashboard na-ebute ụzọ.

| Mgbanwe                                 | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(ịchọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_    | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara nke ọma maka ọrụ npm, systemd na PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(ịchọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_    | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Nhọrọ ndabere nkịtị maka ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ebe njedebe CDP nke ime; Docker na-eji sidecar dị na ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Ọ bụrụ na edobere ya, arịrịọ ọ bụla e zigara CDP proxy sidecar ga-ezipụkwa uru a n'isiokwu `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` (#13679). Ọ bụrụ na enweghị uru, proxy ahụ ga-ebufe arịrịọ na-enweghị nyocha njirimara — n'ọnọdụ ahụ, naanị nkewapụ netwọk nke netwọk Compose `chatgpt-web-codex-net` na-echebe ya. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya. |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | ID tunnel OpenAI zuru ụwa ọnụ maka okirikiri ngwaọrụ Codex mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Igodo runtime tunnel zuru ụwa ọnụ; egbula ya mgbe ọ bụla na ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Aha ziri ezi nke njikọ ahaziri iche ChatGPT e kere ọhụrụ maka àkwà mmiri MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ndekọ pụrụ iche maka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị, broker na tunnel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, a na-echekwa onyonyo nyocha ihe nchọgharị n'ebe nlele ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na binary launcher na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(ịchọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_    | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na binary runtime Bun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Nhọrọ ndabere legacy maka `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; nhazi ọhụrụ na-eji aha kanọnịkal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

## Àkwà Mmiri OmniConductor

Onye nnata SSE na-adịte aka nke na-eme ka ọrụ hub OmniConductor kwekọọ na A2A TaskManager mpaghara (`src/lib/conductor/`). Ọ bụ nke a na-ahọrọ iji — àkwà mmiri ahụ na-amalite naanị mgbe edobere `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Ọ bụ naanị n'akụkụ sava: token hub agaghị erute na ihe nchọgharị ma ọlị.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                 | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                | Nkọwa                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(oghere)_              | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL ntọala nke hub OmniConductor (dịka `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya = àkwà mmiri agbanyụrụ.      |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(oghere)_              | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Nzere hub maka feed SSE — mepụta peer nke ụdị `spokesperson` na hub (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).                    |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(oghere)_              | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Nzere maka inyefe ọrụ A2A→hub na-abata (`POST /v1/tasks`); ọ na-eji `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL ntọala nke ọrụ ọnụ na-ekwuchitere (Faro) dị n'azụ proxy nkata dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).               |

### Nhazi oge na-eburu oke n'uche

`open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` na-eji nke a maka nyocha mmefu token tupu arịrịọ. Ọ bụ nke a na-ahọrọ iji — omume routing ndabara anaghị agbanwe mgbe edoghị ya.

| Mgbanwe                         | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------- | ------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING` | `0`     | `open-sse/services/combo.ts` | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, mafere njikọ ndị mmefu token ha n'ime windo ọ bụla (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tebụl `provider_quota_state`) na-enweghị ike ịkwụ ụgwọ atụmatụ ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ tupu izipu ya. Ọ na-ekwe ka arịrịọ gafee mgbe enweghị mmefu e haziri. |
